Re-auditing refines the finding: inertness decomposes by cause into structural inertness (antipodal-pair geometry, present in good SAEs) and competitive inertness (a TopK pathology of degraded SAEs), and by direction into read- and write-inertness, which five antipodal pairs dissociate completely -- unmonitorable yet steerable through the same atom, with steering specificities of 143-310 attached to zero ablation effects. We document why byte-exact reproducibility is unavailable by construction, and propose reporting it as a stack of claims with explicit scopes. Applying the instrument to a production SAE reproduces the pattern at small scale (14% inert) and surfaces an atom-collision signal: a handful of atoms recur as the nearest match for dozens of unrelated concepts, replicated across three batches.

</details>


### [176] [Forgetful Attention: A Trainable Support-Vector Memory with Certified Selection and Exact Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12204)
*Vishwajith Ramesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出支持向量注意力（SV-Attention），一种基于一类SVM的最大间隔记忆机制，能够认证删除token后输出不变，并支持精确删除路径的增量求解。在fp64实验中，删除与重新训练恢复相同的最优分区，决策函数中位数偏差约10^-9；在MIMIC-IV流数据上，认证选择达到0.86 vs 0.32的稀有项召回率；在enwik8上获得2.178 BPC，相比滑动窗口Transformer提升8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时记忆（如注意力机制）无法在删除某个token后认证输出不变，也无法彻底移除其影响，缺乏可验证的因果遗忘能力。

Method: 将注意力建模为带固定框参数C的一类SVM，其支持系数作为记忆权重；通过活动集划分将保留token赋予零权重，实现可认证的删除；提出可逆增量求解器，在删除token后恢复与重新训练一致的状态。

Result: fp64实验中，删除与重训练恢复相同分区，决策函数中位数偏差约10^-9（学习键上10^-13）；最坏情况10^-2限于病态重复样本。训练速度达9,125 tokens/s（3.22M参数模型），比MPS softmax参考慢35.8倍。认证选择稀有项召回率0.86 vs 0.32，退化小时保留0.80 vs 0.05。enwik8上2.178 BPC vs 2.383，提升8.6%（p=0.001）。

Conclusion: SV-Attention提供了可认证的token删除、外科手术式遗忘和精确编辑能力，在长序列和遗忘场景下显著优于标准注意力机制，但训练速度仍有较大差距。

Abstract: Attention can be viewed as an online learner over context, yet existing test-time memories cannot certify that dropping a token leaves outputs unchanged or delete its influence outright. We introduce Support Vector Attention (SV-Attention), a max-margin memory whose weights are support coefficients of a one-class SVM with fixed box parameter C. Its active-set partition gives reserve tokens exactly zero weight, certifying output-preserving eviction; a reversible incremental solver deletes a token to recover the state produced by retraining without it under the same C. In fp64 experiments, decrement and refit recover identical partitions whenever the optimum is unique, and their decision functions match to a median deviation of about 10^-9 (10^-13 on learned keys); the 10^-2 worst case is confined to ill-conditioned duplicates and remains below coefficient decay in every regime. The exact path reuses the maintained KKT inverse in a custom backward. Training uses a separate stabilized batched approximation and does not carry the exact-deletion certificate; it reaches 9,125 tokens/s on a 3.22M-parameter model, while remaining 35.8 times slower than an MPS softmax reference. At matched budgets, certified selection reaches 0.86 vs. 0.32 rare-item recall and retains 0.80 vs. 0.05 deterioration hours on real MIMIC-IV streams. We also demonstrate surgical forgetting, exact editing, patient-record deletion, and a forgettable retrieval memory over real sentence embeddings. On enwik8, the hybrid obtains 2.178 BPC vs. 2.383 for a matched-state sliding-window Transformer across seven seeds (8.6% paired improvement, p=0.001); a three-seed TinyStories result is directionally positive but not significant (p=0.057).

</details>


### [177] [Speculate with Memory: Lossless Acceleration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12236)
*Yu Li,Qinyuan Ye,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Jiaxin Zhang,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带有三种在线记忆系统的投机执行方法，通过从历史轨迹中学习来提升LLM智能体的预测质量，在六个基准测试上实现了19-39%的动作预测准确率提升和最高2.5倍的观测预测速度提升，且完全无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投机执行方法是无状态的，在任务间丢弃所有信息，导致预测质量无法随经验积累而提升。

Method: 为投机器配备三种在线记忆系统：对比转移表（追踪动作序列统计）、情景记忆（检索上下文相似片段）和混淆追踪器（抑制重复错误）。

Result: 在六个基准测试上，动作预测准确率提升19-39%，观测预测任务提升高达2.5倍，且收益随记忆积累持续增长。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的投机执行是零额外开销、完全无损的加速方法，其预测质量能随经验积累持续提升。

Abstract: Speculative execution accelerates LLM agents by using a smaller, cheaper model to predict and pre-launch the next step while the environment is idle. However, existing speculators are stateless and discard all information between tasks, preventing prediction quality from improving with experience. We equip the speculator with three online memory systems that learn from past agent trajectories: a contrastive transition table tracking action-sequence statistics, an episodic memory retrieving contextually similar segments, and a confusion tracker suppressing recurring errors. We evaluate this approach on six benchmarks spanning three speculation types: action prediction, observation prediction, and chained prediction. Memory-augmented speculation yields a 19--39\% relative accuracy improvement on action prediction and up to a $2.5\times$ increase on observation prediction tasks with repetitive action spaces. These gains grow continuously as memory accumulates and generalize across speculator models of varying cost. All speculation is lossless because it runs during idle time at zero added wall-clock cost, and the actor's trajectory is identical to non-speculative execution.

</details>


### [178] [Cluster-Weighted EDMD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12243)
*Lorenzo Tomaz,Judd Rosenblatt,Flavio Kicis,Thomas B. Jones,Diogo Schwerz de Lucena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD)方法，通过联合学习软相空间划分和每个簇的EDMD算子，在Lorenz、阻尼摆和Duffing系统上相比传统EDMD显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准EDMD使用单个全局Koopman算子来处理所有状态空间区域，但当不同区域呈现不同局部动力学特性时效率低下，需要一种能够自适应地学习局部Koopman模型的方法。

Method: CW-EDMD通过期望最大化(EM)目标联合学习软相空间划分和每个簇的EDMD算子，其EM目标基于几何邻近性和预测残差来分配每次转移，使簇专门化于局部Koopman模型准确的区域而非数据密集的区域。

Result: 在Lorenz、阻尼摆和Duffing系统上，跨越36种配置和10个随机种子，CW-EDMD在一步预测和5秒滚动预测上均优于同阶EDMD。在288组成对比较中，258个案例有显著误差降低，4个增加，26个无差异。中位一步误差降低分别为摆系统57倍、Duffing系统2.7倍、Lorenz系统12倍。

Conclusion: CW-EDMD通过自适应局部Koopman建模显著提升了Koopman算子的逼近精度，是一种有效的多模态动力学系统数据驱动建模方法。

Abstract: Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition (EDMD) approximates Koopman operators from data, but a single global operator is inefficient when different state-space regions exhibit distinct local dynamics. We introduce Cluster-Weighted EDMD (CW-EDMD), which jointly learns a soft phase-space partition and a per-cluster EDMD operator. Its Expectation-Maximization (EM) objective assigns each transition based on both geometric proximity and prediction residuals, so clusters specialize where local Koopman models are accurate rather than where the data are dense. On Lorenz, damped pendulum, and Duffing systems, across 36 configurations and 10 seeds, CW-EDMD improves matched-degree EDMD in one-step and 5s-rollout prediction. Across 288 paired comparisons, there are significant error reductions in 258 cases, increases in 4, and no differences in 26. Median one-step error reductions are 57x, 2.7x, and 12x on pendulum, Duffing, and Lorenz, respectively.

</details>


### [179] [Proximity Features: Privacy-Compliant Cold-Start Personalization at Airbnb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12246)
*Wei Jiang,Bin Xu,Hui Gao,Bharathi Thangamani,Weiwei Guo,Sundar Srinivasavaradhan,Tracy Yu,Huiji Gao,Michael Kinoti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为Proximity Features的隐私合规特征系统，通过地理邻近性聚类（约1000个相邻用户为一组）生成聚合用户信号，解决了Airbnb等双边市场中因用户历史数据缺失（如未登录用户、首次访客）导致的个性化推荐难题。该系统利用地理IP数据和自适应聚类算法，无需持久化个体标识符，已在Airbnb生产环境中部署，在线A/B实验显示显著提升预订量，尤其对无历史或历史过时的用户效果最为明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双边市场平台中，对于高决策成本、低频购买场景，大量用户（如未登录用户、首次访客）缺乏足够的交互历史，导致传统用户级特征无法用于个性化推荐。同时，隐私法规和第三方Cookie限制进一步加剧了基于标识符追踪的困难。

Method: 通过地理IP数据和自适应聚类算法，将地理位置邻近的用户聚合成约1000人的群体，生成聚合用户级信号。系统仅在用户同意范围内基于聚合数据进行操作，推理时无需持久化个体标识符，从设计上保障隐私合规。

Result: 系统已在Airbnb生产环境部署，服务于营销落地页、目的地推荐等多个场景，在线A/B实验显示预订量统计显著提升，其中无历史或历史过时用户获益最大。

Conclusion: Proximity Features通过地理邻近性聚合方法，在无需个体标识符的前提下有效缓解了冷启动用户的个性化推荐问题，兼顾了隐私合规与业务效果提升。

Abstract: Personalization in two-sided marketplaces relies heavily on user-level features, yet for platforms with infrequent, high-consideration purchases, a large fraction of users lack sufficient history for effective recommendation, spanning both paid and organic channels. At Airbnb, a substantial share of search requests comes from logged-out or first-time users, with this challenge especially pronounced on paid-channel landing pages, leaving traditional user-level features unavailable for a large fraction of traffic. Privacy regulations and increasing restrictions on third-party cookies further limit identifier-based tracking for non-essential use cases. This paper introduces Proximity Features, a privacy-compliant feature system that groups users by geographic proximity using geo-IP data and an adaptive clustering algorithm, producing aggregated user-level signals for groups of approximately 1,000 nearby users without requiring a persistent individual identifier at inference time. Privacy is preserved by design: the pipeline operates on consented, aggregated data only within consent-gated privacy controls.
  The system is deployed in production at Airbnb, serving multiple surfaces including marketing landing pages and destination recommendation, with engagement emails integration under way. Online A/B experiments demonstrate statistically significant lifts in bookings, with the largest gains observed among users with absent or stale history.

</details>


### [180] [Understanding Structured Health Data through Interaction-Aware Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12255)
*Ji Hwan Park,Ying Ding,Tianjin Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于交互感知的混合专家模型，利用结构化健康记录的多层级视图进行中风后僵硬预测。尽管性能提升有限，但路由归因揭示了不同视图间的系统性重要性差异，强调视图构建对可解释性的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何利用结构化健康记录的多层级视图，通过交互感知的混合专家模型提升中风后僵硬预测的准确性和可解释性。

Method: 采用交互感知的混合专家模型（mixture-of-experts），结合多层级视图（multi-level views）的结构化健康记录进行分析。

Result: 模型性能提升有限，但路由归因分析发现不同视图之间存在系统性的重要性差异。

Conclusion: 视图构建是影响模型可解释性的关键因素，尽管性能增益不显著。

Abstract: We study interaction-aware mixture-of-experts for post-stroke rigidity prediction using multi-level views of structured health records. Despite minimal performance gains, routing attribution reveals systematic importance differences across views, underscoring view construction as key to interpretability.

</details>


### [181] [TerraZero: Procedural Driving Simulation for Zero-Demonstration Self-Play at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13028)
*Zhouchonghao Wu,Akshay Rangesh,Weixin Li,Wei-Jer Chang,Zachary Lee,Tim Wang,Wei Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TerraZero是一个程序化驾驶模拟器与自对弈训练框架，通过CPU模拟+GPU推理的零拷贝架构实现130万agent步/秒的吞吐量，完全基于强化学习从零训练策略，无需人类演示或后备规划器，首次让纯学习策略登顶InterPlan长尾基准，同时在常规驾驶和仿真真实性指标上达到最优或领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶模拟器在训练鲁棒自动驾驶系统时面临三角矛盾：强化学习需要高速仿真，行为落地需要真实地图结构，安全关键长尾场景难以从稀疏的日志数据中获取。需要一种既能大规模加速训练、又能覆盖无限多样化场景的解决方案。

Method: 基于可配置C引擎实现CPU模拟+GPU推理的零拷贝管线，单服务器GPU达130万agent步/秒。将日志数据仅用作地图几何来源，每张地图通过随机化规则型道路参与者、信号控制器以及agent动力学/奖励/尺寸生成无限场景。采用跨GPU的纯强化学习自对弈配方，无人类演示、无推理时后备规划器。

Result: 策略零样本泛化到不同城市和数据集（包括无监督下涌现左侧通行驾驶）；首次以纯学习策略登顶InterPlan长尾基准，超越更大规模的学习型规划器；在常规驾驶val14基准中取得最佳碰撞和碰撞时间得分，位列最优方法之一；在Waymo Open Sim Agents真实性指标上优于其他无演示方法，与最强锚点自对弈方法竞争力相当。

Conclusion: TerraZero证明了不依赖人类演示和后备规划器、仅靠强化学习自对弈即可训练出在长尾安全和常规驾驶上均达到顶尖水平的驾驶策略，统一的框架同时支持自车驾驶策略和多类型仿真智能体，为自动驾驶大规模训练提供了高效、可扩展的范式。

Abstract: Training robust autonomous driving agents requires a simulator that is fast enough for reinforcement learning at scale, realistic enough to ground behavior in real-world map structure, and diverse enough to cover the safety-critical long tail that logged data rarely contains. We present TerraZero, a procedural driving simulator and self-play training stack. A configurable C engine runs simulation on the CPU and policy inference on the GPU over a zero-copy path, sustaining 1.3M agent-steps per second on a single server-grade GPU, far faster than existing object-level simulators, while keeping fidelity lighter single-agent systems omit: heterogeneous agents, multiple dynamics models, and full traffic-rule enforcement. TerraZero treats logged data only as a source of real-world map geometry, populating each map with randomized rule-based road users and signal controllers and randomizing agent dynamics, rewards, and sizes per episode, so a map yields an unbounded set of scenarios. Every reported policy trains from scratch by reinforcement learning alone on a compute-efficient self-play recipe across GPUs, with zero human demonstrations and no fallback planner at inference. Policies generalize zero-shot across cities and datasets, including emergent left-hand-traffic driving without explicit supervision. As an ego policy, TerraZero is the first fully learned policy to top the InterPlan long-tail benchmark, ahead of larger learned planners; on routine-driving val14 it ranks among the best approaches and is the safest, posting the best collision and time-to-collision scores. On Waymo Open Sim Agents realism the same recipe outperforms other demonstration-free methods and is competitive with the strongest reference-anchored self-play method. One stack serves both roles: driving policies across dynamics for cars and trucks, and sim agents that jointly control vehicles, pedestrians, and cyclists.

</details>


### [182] [From Many to Meaningful: Feature-Guided Zero-Shot Chronic Kidney Disease Screening Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12260)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Sirajam Munira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于大型语言模型（LLM）的零样本慢性肾病（CKD）早期筛查框架，无需数据集特定训练，仅使用少量社区可获取的临床特征，在三个跨国数据集上实现了适合筛查的性能水平，为资源受限场景提供了传统机器学习的实用补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于机器学习的CKD筛查方法依赖大量标注数据、资源密集型病理测试或高维临床特征，且对分布漂移的鲁棒性有限，难以在社区和资源受限的筛查场景中部署。因此需要探索一种无需训练、依赖最小特征集的可行筛查方案。

Method: 提出特征引导的零样本框架：1）通过ML分析选择紧凑且临床相关的特征子集；2）将表格患者记录通过标准化提示模板序列化为文本；3）在零样本设置下评估LLaMA-3、Qwen-3、Mistral和GPT-4o-mini四种LLM的性能；4）使用完整特征集和所选子集在三个跨国CKD数据集上评估泛化能力。

Result: 所选特征集在所有模型和数据集上均取得了一致且统计显著的平衡准确率和概率估计改进，达到了适合筛查目的的性能水平。

Conclusion: LLM能够支持无需训练的、临床有意义的CKD筛查，仅使用最小社区可获取的患者特征，可作为传统ML方法在真实筛查场景中的实用补充。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is essential for preventing irreversible progression; however, many machine learning (ML)-based screening methods remain difficult to deploy in community and resource-limited screening settings due to their reliance on large labeled datasets, resource-intensive pathology tests, or high-dimensional clinical features, and limited robustness to population and distributional shifts. This study examines the feasibility of using large language models (LLMs) for early-stage CKD screening in a zero-shot setting, without dataset-specific training. We propose a feature-guided zero-shot framework that evaluates LLM performance using a selected set of clinically meaningful, readily available community-based features, rather than exhaustive clinical inputs. Feature selection was guided by ML-based analysis to identify a compact, clinically relevant subset of variables. Tabular patient records were subsequently serialized into text using standardized prompt templates to enable zero-shot inference. The zero-shot performance of four LLMs (LLaMA-3, Qwen-3, Mistral, and GPT-4o-mini) was evaluated using both the full feature set and the selected subset. Generalizability was assessed across three heterogeneous CKD datasets spanning three countries. Across models and datasets, the selected feature set yielded consistent and statistically significant improvements in balanced accuracy and probability estimates, achieving performance levels suitable for screening purposes. These findings suggest that LLMs can support clinically meaningful, training-free CKD screening using minimal community-accessible patient features, offering a practical complement to conventional ML methods in real-world screening contexts.

</details>


### [183] [Saturation Makes Quantization Error Additive: A Coverage Model with a Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12266)
*Joshua Hill*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过布尔超立方体上的集合函数分析，发现混合精度量化中85-93%的损失方差可由单层效应解释，并提出覆盖模型（coverage model）用于低比特（≤4比特）权重量化配置预测与分配，在30B-355B参数模型上取得了最低的KL散度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合精度量化方法（如HAWQ、CoopQ）假设可由逐层或逐对敏感性独立重构量化损失，但这一假设在4比特权重量化场景下是否成立尚未被系统验证。本文旨在严格检验该前提，并基于实证发现设计更优的量化配置预测与分配方法。

Method: 将量化配置的损失变化视为布尔超立方体上的集合函数，通过两种经典基变换（Fourier/Walsh-Hadamard等）分析其方差结构；提出覆盖模型f(S)=c(1-∏(1-a_i))，拟合逐层break-rate后预测配置损失；提出加性模型作为最优一阶预测器，利用Parseval恒等式计算其MSE下限，作为可解释性证书。

Result: 在4比特精度下，85-93%的损失方差由逐层效应解释；逐层加性项的单调解变换对配置排序的错误率不超过2%。覆盖模型从L个break-rate拟合出的方差分布与实测值相差仅几个百分点。在30B-355B参数模型上，所提分配器在相同内存预算下取得了最低的KL散度；在低于4比特时，基于梯度敏感性的分配已无法产生有效生成，而所提方法仍能完成代码与推理任务。

Conclusion: 逐层效应主导了量化损失的变化，加性模型可作为可靠的一阶近似；覆盖模型在极低比特场景下优于传统梯度敏感性方法，为大规模模型的混合精度量化提供了更鲁棒的分配策略。

Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization must decide which parts of a model to keep at higher precision. A common premise, shared by sensitivity-based methods such as HAWQ and CoopQ, is that the loss from quantizing a set of layers can be reconstructed from per-layer or pairwise sensitivities measured in isolation. We test this premise at the 4-bit weight-and-activation precisions now being deployed, treating the change in loss $f(S)$ from quantizing a layer set $S$ as a set function on the Boolean cube and analyzing it through two classical changes of basis. This analysis yields two findings. First, across configurations drawn from the deployment distribution, 85--93\% of the variance of $f$ is explained by per-layer effects alone. Second, a monotone transform of a sum of per-layer terms reproduces $f$'s ranking of configurations, misordering at most 2\% of pairs.
  We propose the coverage model $f(S)=c\bigl(1-\prod_{i\in S}(1-a_i)\bigr)$, which reproduces the measured variance profile of $f$ to within a few percent from its $L$ fitted break-rates. This structure supports two predictors of a configuration's loss, each with $L+1$ parameters. The additive model is the optimal first-order predictor. By Parseval's identity its mean-squared error equals the variance of $f$ left unexplained by per-layer effects, which we measure on full lattices, estimate out of sample at full-network scale, and report with every result as a certificate of how well any additive model can do. The coverage model itself is the second predictor.
  As allocators at matched memory, they attain the lowest KL divergence among the compared allocators on models from 30B to 355B parameters. Below four bits, the resulting allocations continue to solve code and reasoning tasks at budgets where allocations from gradient sensitivities no longer produce terminating generations.

</details>


### [184] [Track, Rank, Crack: Epistemic Working Memory Scales Multi-Hop Reasoning in Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12267)
*Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SLEUTH，一种通过结构化认知工作记忆（明确维护已确认事实、活跃假设和未解决问题）来缓解语言代理在长链推理中“上下文稀释”问题的方法。实验表明，该方法在多个多跳基准上优于现有基线，且优势随难度增加而扩大。进一步分析揭示了“证据充分性问题”并提出了轻量级承诺触发器，而结构化状态是实现有效承诺的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言代理在长推理链中性能急剧下降，原因是调查状态仅隐式存在于不断增长的上下文窗口中，早期发现被后续检索淹没（上下文稀释）。需要一种显式维护推理状态的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出SLEUTH框架，采用结构化认知工作记忆，包含三个组件：Confirmed Facts（基于来源的已确认事实）、Active Hypotheses（按证据排序的活跃假设）和Open Questions（驱动下一步行动的未解决问题）。此外，引入轻量级承诺触发器解决证据充分性问题。

Result: 在五个多跳基准和五个基线方法上，SLEUTH相比HotpotQA提升+5分，在4跳链上提升+11分，超越无需多轮迭代的Reflexion。在较弱模型上强制协议遵守可带来最多+19分的提升。

Conclusion: 组织推理的方式（结构化认知状态）而非原始模型能力是扩展多跳推理的关键因素。结构化状态是实现有效承诺的必要条件，相同触发器在非结构化代理上无效。

Abstract: Language agents that interleave reasoning and tool use degrade sharply as reasoning chains lengthen, even when each individual step is easy. We trace this to context dilution: an agent's investigative state (what it has confirmed, what it suspects, and what it still needs) lives only implicitly in a growing context window, where early discoveries are buried under later retrievals. We introduce SLEUTH, which makes this state explicit and actionable through a structured epistemic working memory: the agent maintains Confirmed Facts grounded to sources, Active Hypotheses ranked by evidence, and Open Questions that directly drive its next action. Across five multi-hop benchmarks and five established baselines, SLEUTH's advantage grows with difficulty, from +5 points on HotpotQA to +11 on 4-hop chains, surpassing Reflexion without multiple episodes. Analyzing where the remaining gap lies, we identify the evidence sufficiency problem: agents often find the answer but fail to commit, exhausting their budget on needless verification. A lightweight commitment trigger fixes this, but only when the agent already maintains structured state: the identical trigger applied to an unstructured agent yields no improvement, isolating organized epistemic state as the necessary condition for effective commitment. Finally, enforcing protocol adherence on a weaker model recovers up to +19 points on the hardest problems, showing that how an agent organizes its reasoning, not raw model capability, is the active ingredient for scaling multi-hop reasoning.

</details>


### [185] [Quantum Port-Hamiltonian Neural Networks: Learning Conservative and Dissipative Dynamics via Measurement-Induced Nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12269)
*Dibakar Sigdel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子端口-哈密顿神经网络(Q-pHNNs)，一种通过等构哈密顿映射(IHM)将经典动力学编码为参数化量子电路的结构保持方法。IHM将斜对称互联矩阵$\mathbf{J}$映射为酉门演化，将半正定耗散矩阵$\mathbf{R}$映射为基于中间测量和经典前馈的测量诱导非线性(MINL)，从而从结构上保证守恒性和无源性。文章实例化了四种架构，并在非线性摆和阻尼谐振子实验上验证了能量漂移率$1.35\%$、能量单调性$100\%$及阻尼系数识别误差$12.1\%$等性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经典神经常微分方程和物理信息神经网络在长时间模拟中难以严格保持守恒律和无源性，而量子计算天然具有酉演化特性，有望从结构层面保证系统的物理属性。

Method: 提出等构哈密顿映射(IHM)，将端口-哈密顿系统的$\mathbf{J}$矩阵映射为量子酉门，将$\mathbf{R}$矩阵映射为中间测量+经典前馈(MINL)；设计了四种架构：量子HNN、基于Born规则测量的Q-pHNN、联合学习能量和阻尼的Q-pHNN、拓扑纠缠量子图神经网络。

Result: 非线性摆和阻尼谐振子实验显示：使用辛积分器和尺度校正后相对能量漂移率仅$1.35\%$；MINL电路实现$100\%$能量单调性；从向量场快照中识别阻尼系数的误差为$12.1\%$，且无需直接监督阻尼系数。

Conclusion: Q-pHNN通过量子电路的固有结构和测量机制从设计上保证了经典动力学的守恒性和无源性，避免了惩罚项方法，为量子机器学习在物理模拟中的应用提供了新的结构保持框架。

Abstract: We introduce Quantum Port-Hamiltonian Neural Networks (Q-pHNNs), a family of parameterised quantum circuits that learn classical dynamics in a structure-preserving manner. The framework relies on the Isomorphic Hamiltonian Mapping (IHM): the skew-symmetric interconnection matrix $\mathbf{J}$ corresponds to unitary gate evolution, and the positive-semidefinite dissipation matrix $\mathbf{R}$ corresponds to Measurement-Induced NonLinearity (MINL) realised via mid-circuit measurement and classical feedforward. This ensures conservation and passivity are enforced by construction rather than penalty terms. We instantiate the IHM in four architectures: (1) a Quantum HNN that learns conservative energy manifolds and extracts Hamilton's equations exactly via the Parameter-Shift Rule; (2) a Q-pHNN using Born-rule measurement for dissipation; (3) a Q-pHNN jointly learning the energy ansatz and damping coefficient; and (4) a topology-entangled Quantum Graph Neural Network for $N$-node coupled-phasor networks. Experiments on the nonlinear pendulum and damped harmonic oscillator demonstrate: (i)~$1.35\%$ relative energy drift with a symplectic integrator and scale correction; (ii)~$100\%$ energy monotonicity for the MINL circuit; and (iii)~$12.1\%$ error in damping-coefficient identification from vector-field snapshots with no direct supervision on the damping coefficient.

</details>


### [186] [A hybrid analytical-PINN model for subsurface simulation of geothermal heat exchangers in heterogeneous underground](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12271)
*Moke Rao,Thomas Hamacher,Smajil Halilovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数化物理信息神经网络（PINN），用于求解带有地埋管换热器（BHEs）作为奇异源的非均质土壤热问题。通过解析线源模型消除奇异性，利用显式梯度热导率公式实现物理信息学习，并利用叠加原理将学习到的校正作为通用校正器。数值实验验证了该方法在三种不同解析模型下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决带有地埋管换热器（BHEs）作为奇异源的非均质土壤热问题中，传统数值方法难以处理奇异性问题以及非均质参数化学习的挑战。

Method: (i) 采用解析线源模型自然消除奇异性；(ii) 使用显式梯度热导率公式进行物理信息学习；(iii) 通过叠加原理将学习到的校正作为通用校正器。具体包括：温度变化分解、将非均质响应转化为校正补偿问题、源项奇异性的排除、将原问题转化为带均匀初始条件的校正扩散/对流-扩散方程、使用线性变化热导率建模土壤非均质性、以单位取热速率单井的通用校正器为学习目标、最小化物理信息与数据锚定的损失函数、将源位置指示函数作为网络特征输入。

Result: 基于三种不同的解析模型进行数值测试，结果表明所提方法在求解非均质土壤热问题中具有有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的参数化物理信息神经网络能够有效处理带有奇异源的非均质土壤热问题，通过奇异性消除、参数化学习和通用校正器机制，实现了高效且准确的计算框架。

Abstract: In this paper, a parametric physics-informed neural network for solving the heterogeneous soil thermal problem with borehole heat exchangers (BHEs) as singular sources is developed. There are three novel features in the present framework; namely, (i) the singularity is naturally removed by using analytical line source models; (ii) using the explicit formulation for gradient thermal conductivity enables physics-informed learning of the parametrization featuring the conductivity; (iii) the learned correction is utilized as an efficient universal corrector via superposition principles. We first introduce the decomposition of the temperature change and transform the approximation of the entire heterogeneous response to the correction compensating the difference between the practical solution and idealized homogeneous approximation. In such a way, the delta function singularity is excluded and the bulk heat transfer is captured for the sake of facilitating the effective training of the neural network. The original problem is then reformulated as a governing correction diffusion or advection-diffusion equation subject to a homogeneous initial condition. The linearly varying thermal conductivity is used to model the soil heterogeneity. We propose a physics-informed neural network to approximate a universal corrector with respect to a single borehole with unit heat extraction rate. As a result, the network is trained by minimizing the physics-informed and data-anchored loss function that is evaluated for sampled conductivity parameters on adaptively selected training points. In addition, we include the location indicator function regarding the source as a feature input of network and find that it helps the network to process the local information. We perform numerical tests to exhibit the effectiveness of the proposed method based on three different analytical models.

</details>


### [187] [Gradient Flow Dynamics and Implicit Bias of Diagonal Linear Networks under Infinitesimal Initialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12332)
*Jiajie Zhao,Jianxing Wang,Junjie Yang,Zhiwei Bai,Yaoyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了对角线性网络在无穷小初始化下的梯度流动力学，将Pesme & Flammarion (2023)的定理1推广到深层对角线性网络和更广泛的两层对角线性网络类别。证明这些模型的训练轨迹可由所提算法1等价刻画，且该算法收敛到修正的ℓ1范数最小化问题的解。此外，揭示了结构不变流形(SIM)作为关键几何结构塑造学习过程的内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度对角线性网络在无穷小初始化下的隐式偏置，并将现有关于两层网络的理论分析推广到更一般的网络架构。

Method: 通过定理推广和算法等价性证明，将训练轨迹映射为一种新的算法(Algorithm 1)，并证明其收敛到修正的ℓ1范数最小化问题。利用结构不变流形(SIM)作为几何分析工具。

Result: 两种网络架构在无穷小初始化下的隐式偏置对应于修正的ℓ1范数；训练动力学可由Algorithm 1等价描述。

Conclusion: 无穷小初始化下，深层与广义两层对角线性网络的隐式偏置统一为修正的ℓ1范数最小化，且SIM是关键几何结构。

Abstract: We study the gradient flow dynamics of diagonal linear networks for regression tasks under infinitesimal initialization. Extending Theorem 1 from Pesme & Flammarion (2023), we generalize the analysis to both deep diagonal linear networks and a broader class of two-layer diagonal linear networks (as defined in Definition 4.1). Specifically, we demonstrate that the training trajectories of these models can be equivalently characterized by the proposed Algorithm 1. We further prove that this algorithm converges to the solution of a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm minimization problem. As a result, we establish that the implicit bias of both network architectures corresponds to a modified $ \mathcal{l}_1 $ norm in the regime of infinitesimal initialization. Additionally, we provide insights into the underlying mechanisms governing these dynamics by identifying the Structural Invariant Manifold (SIM) (Zhao et al., 2026) as the key geometric structure that shapes the learning process.

</details>


### [188] [Generating Developable 3D Molecules via Pocket-Conditioned Diffusion and Property-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12349)
*Ruoxi Gao,Jiangweizhi Peng,Ziqi Chen,Frazier N. Baker,David C. Kombo,John L. Kane,Andrew A. Scholte,Yi Li,Matthew J. LaMarche,Luigi I. Iconaru,Hans-Peter Biemann,Mingyi Hong,Xia Ning*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了 conDitar-dev，一个条件扩散驱动的基于结构的药物设计框架，包含多尺度口袋表征学习（msPRL）、口袋条件扩散模型（conDitar）和生成时优化模块（paOPT），在人类疾病靶点基准上实现了-8.85 kcal/mol的平均结合得分，并在五个ADMET属性上相较conDitar实现高达73%的提升。实验验证中，针对PD-L1生成的两个分子展示出3.49和3.75 μM的SPR KD值，针对CSF1R设计的分子经扩展后获得IC50低至200 nM的选择性抑制剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的SBDD方法存在三个主要问题：1）口袋与配体表示学习耦合，限制了表征能力；2）仅建模原子层面的相互作用，忽略了残基层面的关键信息；3）过度关注结合亲和力，忽视了ADMET等成药性属性。因此，需要一种能够同时生成强结合亲和力和良好ADMET属性的分子的方法。

Method: 方法由三个模块组成：1）msPRL：预训练的多尺度口袋表征学习模块，从原子和残基两个层次学习口袋表示；2）conDitar：受msPRL表示引导的口袋条件扩散模型，用于生成配体分子；3）paOPT：生成时优化方法，通过后处理调整生成分子的ADMET属性。

Result: 在新构建的人类疾病靶点基准上，conDitar的平均结合得分为-8.85 kcal/mol，优于现有SBDD基线。在五个ADMET属性上，conDitar-dev相较conDitar性能提升高达73%。针对PD-L1靶点，两个直接生成的分子的SPR KD值分别为3.49和3.75 μM。针对CSF1R靶点，基于设计分子的扩展获得了IC50低至200 nM的选择性抑制剂。

Conclusion: conDitar-dev证明了条件扩散模型在生成兼具强结合力和良好成药性的分子方面的有效性，并且通过湿实验验证了其实际应用价值。该框架不仅能够直接生成高活性的先导化合物，还通过分子扩展发现了药物重定位的机会，为基于结构的药物设计提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Drug discovery and development is time-consuming and resource-intensive, motivating computational approaches such as diffusion models for de novo drug design. Many such models follow the structure-based drug design (SBDD) paradigm, generating molecules to fit a target binding pocket. However, existing diffusion-based SBDD methods typically couple pocket and ligand representation learning, model interactions only at the atom level, and prioritize binding affinity over other developability properties. Here, we introduce conDitar-dev, a conditional diffusion-based SBDD framework for generating ligands with strong binding affinities and favorable ADMET properties. It consists of three modules: msPRL, a pretrained multi-scale pocket representation learning module; conDitar, a pocket-conditioned diffusion model guided by msPRL representations; and paOPT, a generation-time method for optimizing ligand developability. On a newly curated benchmark of human disease targets, conDitar outperforms state-of-the-art SBDD baselines, achieving an average binding score of -8.85 kcal/mol. Across five ADMET properties, conDitar-dev improves performance by up to 73% over conDitar. To further validate the abilities of conDitar-dev to generate developable molecules, we have applied it to two validated druggable targets: programmed death-ligand 1 (PD-L1) and colony-stimulating factor 1 receptor (CSF1R) proteins. Top-ranked generatively designed molecules and their analogs have been experimentally synthesized and biologically tested. Two molecules generated directly by conDitar-dev for PD-L1 exhibited SPR-derived $K_D$ values of 3.49 and 3.75 $μ$M, respectively. Hit expansion based on conDitar-dev-designed molecules identified selective CSF1R inhibitors with IC$_{50}$ values as low as 200 nM, while also uncovering opportunities for drug repositioning.

</details>


### [189] [Reducing information dependency does not cause training data privacy. Adversarially non-robust features do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12354)
*Rasmus Torp,Shailen K. Smith,Adam Breuer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“信息依赖驱动训练数据泄露”的主流观点，证明模型对图像重建攻击的暴露主要由对抗鲁棒性相关因素（非稳健特征）引起，而非死记硬背。作者通过三项反直觉实验和提出的AT-AT训练方法，揭示了隐私与鲁棒性之间新的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战当前主流观点——即信息依赖（包括死记硬背）是导致训练数据遭受图像重建攻击泄露的主要原因，并提出新的解释机制。

Method: 通过三项反直觉实验：（1）防御方法虽降低MIA重建成功率但未降低信息依赖指标HSIC；（2）最大程度记忆数据的模型仍对MIA重建鲁棒；（3）在极端条件下（97%像素未见过）模型仍可被重建。引入AT-AT（Anti Adversarial Training）训练范式主动学习非稳健特征。

Result: 发现MIA下的隐私泄露源于对抗样本领域所称的“非稳健特征”；现有MIA防御通过无意中使模型偏向此类特征获得隐私改进；AT-AT在防御重建攻击和准确率上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 修正了关于训练数据泄露的主流理解，揭示了隐私与鲁棒性之间存在一种新的权衡关系。

Abstract: In this paper, we challenge the prevailing view that information dependency (including rote memorization) drives training data exposure to image reconstruction attacks. We show that extensive exposure can persist without rote memorization and is instead caused by a tunable connection to adversarial robustness. We begin by presenting three surprising results: (1) recent defenses that inhibit reconstruction by Model Inversion Attacks (MIAs), which evaluate leakage under an idealized attacker, do not reduce standard measures of information dependency (HSIC); (2) models that maximally memorize their training datasets remain robust to MIA reconstruction; and (3) models trained without seeing 97% of the training pixels, where recent information-theoretic bounds give arbitrarily strong privacy guarantees under standard assumptions, can still be devastatingly reconstructed by MIA.
  To explain these findings, we provide causal evidence that privacy under MIA arises from what the adversarial examples literature calls ``non-robust'' features (generalizable but imperceptible and unstable features). We further show that recent MIA defenses obtain their privacy improvements by unintentionally shifting models toward such features. To establish this causal relationship, we introduce Anti Adversarial Training (AT-AT), a training regime that intentionally learns non-robust features to obtain both superior reconstruction defense and higher accuracy than state-of-the-art defenses. Our results revise the prevailing understanding of training data exposure and reveal a new privacy-robustness tradeoff.

</details>


### [190] [Same Loss, Same Noise, Opposite Schedules: Noise Structure and Optimizer Normalization Jointly Determine Whether Learning-Rate Cooldown Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12360)
*Subham Singh,Ashutosh Mishra,Subha Raut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上解释了WSD学习率调度中cooldown阶段何时有效：在梯度乘法噪声下，SGD因梯度自衰减而无需cooldown，而符号/归一化方法（如自适应优化器）需要cooldown将学习率降至零才能达到最优；给出了signSGD的平稳分布闭合解，并证明了该现象在动量、重尾噪声下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSD学习率调度在大型模型预训练中已成为默认设置，但cooldown阶段在某些设置下降低最终训练损失，在其他设置下却无效。现有文献缺乏对这一现象的理论解释，本文旨在给出严密的理论分析。

Method: 基于强凸目标函数，在乘法（梯度成比例）噪声和加性噪声假设下，分别分析SGD、signSGD和归一化方法的收敛行为。推导signSGD在二次型上的稳态分布闭合解；利用$(L_0,L_1)$-光滑性证明局部解耦；通过尺度不变性论证将结果推广到d>1维归一化SGD；并验证对动量和重尾噪声的鲁棒性。

Result: 在乘法噪声下，SGD以常数学习率几何收敛，无需cooldown；而signSGD和归一化方法会停留在$O(\eta^2)$的噪声平台上，只有当学习率驱动到零时才能达到最优。加性噪声下所有方法都会产生噪声平台。

Conclusion: cooldown是否有效取决于梯度噪声结构和优化器是否归一化更新步长。内部cooldown的比例选择（如按总步数的固定比例）实际上落入了稳态的"景观-噪声"几何之外，需要更细致的理论指导。

Abstract: The cooldown phase of a warmup-stable-decay (WSD) learning-rate schedule, now a default in large-model pretraining, lowers the final training loss in some settings and does nothing in others. We give a provable account of which case obtains, and it turns on two properties together: the structure of the gradient noise and whether the optimizer normalizes its update. On a strongly convex objective with multiplicative (gradient-proportional) noise, stochastic gradient descent contracts geometrically at a constant learning rate, so cooldown has nothing to improve. Under the same objective and noise, sign-based and normalized methods, the standard surrogates for adaptive optimizers, settle on a noise floor of order $η^2$ and reach the minimizer only as the learning rate is driven to zero; any additive noise then reinstates a floor for every method. The mechanism is elementary: an SGD step shrinks in proportion to the gradient and so anneals itself, whereas a normalized step keeps unit scale and cannot. We solve the signSGD stationary law on the quadratic exactly and obtain the floor constant in closed form, prove a local form of the dissociation under $(L_0,L_1)$-smoothness, extend the floor to normalized SGD in dimension d>1 by a scale-invariance argument, and establish robustness to momentum and heavy-tailed noise. Simulation confirms every prediction, and we demonstrate the resulting noise-regime diagnostic on a real classification task with directly measured gradient noise. The mechanism explains whether cooldown helps; the interior cooldown fraction used at scale lies outside stationary landscape-and-noise geometry.

</details>


### [191] [SinAE: A Single-Architecture Flow-Matching Autoencoder for Cross-Domain Atomic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12380)
*Yuxuan Ren,Fan Yang,Jianhua Yao,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinAE是一个统一的流匹配自编码器，使用普通Transformer架构，无需等变、图或领域特定算子，即可在分子、晶体和蛋白质三个领域实现近无损重建和强生成性能，并展示了跨领域迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子、晶体和蛋白质的生成模型长期各自为政，采用不同的图、等变或基于框架的架构，导致跨领域训练困难且每个领域面临数据稀缺问题。

Method: 提出SinAE，使用普通Transformer编码器和解码器，通过迭代流匹配解码器将重建负担从编码器转移到解码器，实现近无损重建；潜在空间支持标准的扩散Transformer先验进行生成。

Result: 重建误差相比先前潜在基线降低了数个数量级，在分子、晶体和蛋白质生成基准上达到强性能；联合分子-晶体训练同时提升两个领域的表现，证明了跨领域迁移。

Conclusion: 共享的原子级潜在表示能够有效桥接不同3D原子结构领域，实现跨领域知识迁移，为统一3D结构生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its Small molecules, crystals, and proteins all reduce to atoms in 3D space, yet their generative pipelines remain fragmented across domains, each with its own graph, equivariant, or frame-based architecture. Cross-domain training would mitigate per-domain data scarcity, but direct generation in 3D coordinate space cannot easily handle the heterogeneous structural priors of all three domains, and no prior latent autoencoder is simultaneously lossless and architecturally general across all three. We introduce SinAE, a single-architecture flow-matching autoencoder for molecules, crystals, and proteins, with vanilla Transformer encoder and decoder and no equivariant, graph, or domain-specific operators. Rather than requiring the encoder to capture fine-grained geometry, SinAE shifts the reconstruction burden into an iterative flow-matching decoder, achieving near-lossless reconstruction across domains and reducing reconstruction errors by orders of magnitude relative to prior latent baselines. The same per-token latent supports a standard Diffusion Transformer prior that reaches strong performance on molecular, crystal, and protein generation benchmarks. Joint molecule--crystal training strictly improves both domains, providing direct evidence of cross-domain transfer through a shared atomic latent. Code is available at https://github.com/BlueWhaleLab/SinAE .

</details>


### [192] [Differentiable Clone-Structured Causal Graphs for End-to-End Cognitive Map Learning from Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12382)
*Arash Nikzad,Sasan Sarbishegi,Ali Dasmeh,Muhammad Asif,Parsa Gharavi,Erik Husom,Sagar Sen,Andrew B. Lehr,Olivier Penacchio,Ana Clemente,Tristan M. Stöber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出gradCSCG，将Clone-Structured Causal Graph (CSCG) 算法重新构建为完全可微分的模块，并与VQ-VAE感知前端结合，实现了从原始图像序列端到端学习可解释的结构化环境地图，解决了传统CSCG需要预定义离散字母表且难以与神经网络结合的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CSCG作为一种规范的海马体模型能够从混淆观测中学习可解释的地图，但其需要预定义的离散字母表，且期望最大化形式难以与现有神经网络模块结合，无法实现从原始图像序列的端到端处理。作者旨在移除这一障碍。

Method: 1) 将CSCG重新表述为完全可微分的模块gradCSCG；2) 与学习型向量量化变分自编码器(VQ-VAE)感知前端耦合；3) 设计软发射前向传播使得地图学习目标能够反向传播到感知模块；4) 引入损失平衡机制以缓解联合训练中的模块崩溃问题。

Result: 1) 梯度训练在原始符号网格世界中复现了CSCG的结果，从高度混淆观测中恢复房间拓扑；2) 在MNIST图像序列上地图恢复保持鲁棒，每个位置访问均得到新采样的数字图像；3) 在四个高度混淆环境中，端到端流水线成功从视觉输入中恢复底层邻接图，具有高边精确率和召回率。

Conclusion: 本文证明了CSCG可以作为深度学习架构中一个可组合的构建模块，为实现从原始感官输入中端到端学习结构化世界地图提供了原理验证。

Abstract: How can an agent build a structured map of its world from nothing but an ongoing sequence of raw sensory input and its own movements, especially when natural variation means exact sensory patterns rarely repeat? The Clone-Structured Causal Graph algorithm (CSCG), a normative hippocampus model, shows how an interpretable map can be learned from aliased observations. However, CSCG requires a predefined discrete alphabet, and its expectation-maximization formulation is not easily combined with existing neural network modules, preventing the end-to-end processing of raw image sequences. We remove this barrier by reformulating CSCG as a single, fully differentiable module, gradCSCG, and coupling it to a learned vector-quantized variational autoencoder (VQ-VAE) perceptual front-end. A soft emission forward pass allows the map-learning objective to flow back into perception, while a set of loss-balancing mechanisms mitigates module collapse during joint training. We demonstrate, first, that gradient training reproduces CSCG's results on original symbolic grid worlds by recovering room topology from heavily aliased observations. Second, we show that map recovery remains robust on MNIST image sequences, where each visit to a location yields a newly sampled image of its assigned digit. Across four heavily aliased environments, the end-to-end pipeline successfully uncovers the underlying adjacency graph with high edge precision and recall, directly from visual input. This work provides a proof of principle that CSCG can serve as a composable building block in a deep learning architecture.

</details>


### [193] [ReDiTT: Retrieval Augmented Conditional Diffusion Transformers for Asynchronous Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12391)
*Saiyue Lyu,Zhitian Zhang,Ruizhi Deng,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ReDiTT，一种检索增强的条件扩散Transformer模型，用于异步时间序列预测，通过检索结构相似的潜在序列作为条件指导生成，在7个真实数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步时间序列预测中未来事件存在固有不确定性，现有方法难以同时保证长期预测稳定性和样本多样性。

Method: 在潜在空间中运行的条件扩散Transformer，通过检索记忆库中结构相似的潜在序列，利用交叉注意力机制将其作为参考条件注入模型，实现全局结构引导的生成。

Result: 在7个真实数据集上，在下一事件预测和长期预测两项任务中均达到了最先进性能。

Conclusion: 检索增强的条件扩散模型能够有效稳定长期预测并提升样本多样性，为异步时间序列预测提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: We present a diffusion based model for asynchronous time series prediction, where the goal is to predict the next inter event time and event type. To address the inherent uncertainty of future events, we introduce ReDiTT, a retrieval augmented conditional diffusion transformer that operates in latent space. ReDiTT retrieves structurally similar latent sequences from a memory bank during both training and inference and incorporates them as reference conditions through cross attention. This retrieval based conditioning allows the model to attend to relevant temporal dynamics and provides global structural guidance for generation. As a result, ReDiTT stabilizes long horizon forecasting and improves sample diversity. Experiments on seven real world datasets demonstrate state of the art performance on next event prediction and long horizon forecasting. Our code is available at https://github.com/BorealisAI/ReDiTT.

</details>


### [194] [Mechanical Analysis of Parachute Suspension Line Deployment with Binding Tapes Using PINN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12409)
*Xiang Zhao,Ronghui Quan,Yaqi Xiao,Junlin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的算法，用于预测降落伞吊带抽直过程中的张力变化。与传统数值积分方法相比，该算法在计算效率和数值精度上均有提升，并通过飞行试验数据验证了其可靠性。此外，还研究了捆绑带参数对吊带动态张力的影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降落伞吊带抽直是开伞过程的关键初始阶段，涉及复杂的动态载荷变化。现有研究多采用常微分方程数值积分计算吊带张力，但该方法无法快速获取吊带上任意位置的张力值，亟需一种更高效、更精确的预测方法。

Method: 开发了一种物理信息神经网络（PINN）算法，利用物理方程作为约束训练神经网络，用于预测吊带抽直过程中的张力分布。不同于传统数值积分方法，PINN可直接输出任意位置的张力值。

Result: PINN算法在计算效率和数值精度上均优于传统积分方法；通过飞行试验数据和常规数值结果的对比验证，证明了该框架的可靠性和有效性；同时揭示了捆绑带参数对吊带动态张力的调控规律。

Conclusion: PINN算法为降落伞吊带抽直过程中的张力预测提供了一种高效、精确的新方法，克服了传统数值积分方法的局限性，对降落伞系统设计和安全评估具有工程应用价值。

Abstract: Parachutes are widely utilized in aviation, aerospace and lifesaving missions. As the initial stage of parachute deployment, suspension line extraction and straightening directly determines the smooth implementation of subsequent inflation procedures. This ultra-short process involves intricate dynamic load variations. Most existing studies adopt numerical integration of ordinary differential equations to calculate line tension, yet this method fails to rapidly acquire tension values at arbitrary positions along suspension lines. This paper develops a physics-informed neural network (PINN) algorithm for tension prediction during line extraction and straightening, which outperforms traditional integration methods in both computational efficiency and numerical accuracy. Furthermore, the regulatory law of binding tape parameters on line dynamic tension is investigated. Comparative validations against flight test data and conventional numerical results verify the reliability and effectiveness of the proposed PINN framework.

</details>


### [195] [PolarBM: Complex-valued Boltzmann Machine for Modeling Audio Signals in Polar and Log-polar Coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12417)
*Toru Nakashika,Kohei Yatabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolarBM和LogPolarBM，在极坐标下对复值变量建模，显式捕获幅相依赖关系；其受限变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM在音频信号建模中优于传统模型（包括DNN），并适用于无线通信、量子力学等科学工程领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习方法处理复值数据时，常简化为实值框架，忽略了幅值与相位之间的结构关系，导致信息丢失。

Method: 提出PolarBM，在极坐标系下定义复变量的概率密度函数，使相位显式依赖于幅度；进一步提出LogPolarBM，在对数尺度上建模幅度，推导出功率加权非中心复高斯（PW-NCCG）分布，其边缘幅度分布涵盖Rice、Nakagami和非中心卡方分布作为特例；同时引入受限变体PolarRBM和LogPolarRBM。

Result: 实验结果表明，显式建模幅相依赖关系的受限玻尔兹曼机在建模精度上优于传统模型，包括深度神经网络。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型不仅适用于音频信号，还可推广到无线通信、量子力学等涉及复值数据的科学与工程领域。

Abstract: Although vast amounts of data, such as audio signal spectra, are naturally represented using complex numbers, conventional machine learning methods often simplify complex-domain problems by employing frameworks designed for real-valued variables. While this simplification offers computational benefits, it discards structural information regarding the inherent relationship between amplitude and phase. In this paper, we propose a novel Boltzmann machine (BM), named PolarBM, capable of naturally handling complex-valued variables in the polar coordinate (i.e., an amplitude-phase representation). PolarBM defines a probability density function for complex variables in which the phase explicitly depends on the amplitude, thereby capturing the physically important relationships of complex-valued signals. Furthermore, to process audio signals in accordance with human auditory perception, we propose LogPolarBM, which models amplitude on a logarithmic scale. This extension yields a flexible conditional probability density function, a power-weighted noncentral complex Gaussian (PW-NCCG) distribution, whose marginal amplitude distribution encompasses the Rice, Nakagami, and noncentral chi distributions as special cases. For practical applications, we also introduce the restricted variants of these proposed models: PolarRBM and LogPolarRBM. Experimental results demonstrate that by explicitly modeling the dependency between amplitude and phase, the proposed RBMs achieve superior modeling accuracy compared to conventional models, including deep neural networks. Although our experiments focus on audio signals, the utility of the proposed BMs is not limited to audio applications; their potential extends widely across various fields of science and engineering that involve complex-valued data, such as wireless communications and quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [196] [Fisher Rank Inflation: A Spectral Signature of Memorization under Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12438)
*Satwik Bathula,Anand A. Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现带标签噪声的深度网络在训练过程中，其最后一层梯度谱的有效秩会经历先膨胀后收缩的阶段性变化（Fisher Rank Inflation），该现象与记忆化过程一致，可用于检测噪声样本、衡量噪声严重程度，并在多项实验中验证了其稳定性和预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度网络在标签噪声下从学习干净结构到记忆化标签的转变机制，并寻找一个可观测的谱信号来量化这一过程。

Method: 分析每样本最后一层梯度的中心化散度矩阵的谱特征，定义Fisher有效秩；推导一阶留一法归因公式；在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、CIFAR-10N上用SmallCNN、ResNet18、ViT进行实验验证。

Result: 有效秩呈现一致的膨胀-坍塌轨迹；峰值处噪声样本在最高秩贡献样本中占69.2%~96.2%；一阶谱归因接近精确留一法贡献；有效秩随噪声严重度单调上升（28.88→97.09）；秩膨胀起点先于测试性能退化。

Conclusion: Fisher Rank Inflation是一种连接噪声样本富集、噪声严重度和从结构学习到记忆化转变的谱特征，为理解标签噪声下的训练动力学提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Deep networks trained with label noise often learn clean structure before memorizing corrupted labels. We show that this transition leaves a spectral signature in the centered scatter of per-example last-layer gradients. Its effective rank transiently expands during memorization and contracts after corrupted labels are fit. We call this phenomenon Fisher Rank Inflation. Corrupted labels increase effective rank by injecting spectral mass into low-energy or previously unused eigendirections, increasing the entropy of the gradient spectrum. We derive a first-order leave-one-out attribution formula, identify conditions under which corrupted examples contribute more strongly than clean examples, and explain why attribution signals weaken once the normalized Fisher-gradient spectrum stabilizes. We test these predictions on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and CIFAR-10N using SmallCNN, ResNet18, and Vision Transformers. Across settings, Fisher effective rank exhibits a consistent inflation--collapse trajectory aligned with memorization. At peak-rank checkpoints, corrupted examples are enriched among the highest rank-contributing samples, with top-100 noisy fractions from \(69.2\%\) to \(96.2\%\) across five-seed synthetic-corruption experiments and \(94.4\%\pm1.9\%\) on CIFAR-10N. First-order spectral attribution closely matches exact leave-one-out contributions in convolutional models and remains enriched in the Vision Transformer. Peak effective rank increases monotonically with corruption severity, from \(28.88\pm1.95\) under clean training to \(97.09\pm1.78\) at \(60\%\) corruption. In several settings, the retrospectively identified onset of rank inflation precedes observable test degradation. These results establish Fisher Rank Inflation as a spectral signature connecting corrupted-example enrichment, corruption severity, and the transition from structure learning to memorization.

</details>


### [197] [The Computational Basis of Confidence in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12447)
*Dharshan Kumaran,Viorica Patraucean,Maks Ovsanikov,Petar Veličković,Nathaniel Daw*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统计决策置信度（SDC）框架，研究多模态语言模型中答案对数几率（answer logits）是否反映潜在的决策变量（贝叶斯式）还是启发式偏好信号。在感知判别和记忆任务中，答案对数几率差（LD）满足SDC的定性特征（包括folded-X模式），表明其作为决策变量的单调读出器；但在复杂视觉推理中，完整的几何特征消失，揭示了当前框架的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要评估置信度对正确性的预测能力和校准性，但未回答更根本的问题：置信度信号本身代表什么？答案对数几率可能反映足以计算规范置信度的潜在决策变量，也可能是以非贝叶斯方式组合可用证据的启发式偏好信号。作者旨在区分这两种可能性。

Method: 引入计算神经科学的统计决策置信度（SDC）规范框架，将答案对数几率差（LD）作为潜在决策变量的候选读出器，测试SDC预测的定性特征。在三个感知辨别任务、一个记忆决策任务以及复杂视觉推理任务上，对三个多模态非推理模型和一个推理模型进行验证。

Result: 在感知辨别和记忆决策任务中，LD满足SDC的定性特征，包括诊断性的正确/错误折叠X模式（folded-X pattern），表明答案对数几率表现为潜在决策变量的单调读出器而非启发式偏好分数。在复杂视觉推理中，LD仍能预测正确性（超越客观任务难度），但SDC完整的几何特征缺失。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了多模态语言模型中置信度的计算解释，划定了答案对数几率何时表现为潜在决策变量读出器的条件，并建立了SDC作为跨生物智能和人工智能研究置信度的统一框架。

Abstract: Reliable confidence -- the probability that a model's own answer is correct -- is essential for the trustworthy deployment of language models. Existing work has largely evaluated confidence by how well it predicts correctness and whether it is calibrated, leaving open a more fundamental question: what does the confidence signal itself represent? Answer logits may reflect a latent decision variable sufficient to compute normative confidence, or instead a heuristic preference signal that combines the available evidence in a non-Bayesian manner. We address this using statistical decision confidence (SDC), a normative framework from computational neuroscience. Treating the answer-logit difference (LD) as a candidate readout of the latent decision variable, we test the qualitative signatures predicted by SDC. Across three perceptual discrimination tasks and a memory-based decision task, spanning three multimodal non-reasoning models and one reasoning model, LD satisfied these signatures -- including the diagnostic correct/error folded-X pattern -- showing that, in these settings, answer logits behave as monotonic readouts of a latent decision variable rather than heuristic preference scores. In complex visual reasoning, LD continued to predict correctness beyond objective task difficulty, but the full geometric signatures of SDC were absent, illustrating the current boundary of the framework when explicit normative process models are unavailable. These results provide a computational account of confidence in multimodal language models, delineate when answer logits behave as readouts of a latent decision variable, and establish SDC as a unifying framework for studying confidence across biological and artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [198] [Exploring Zero-Shot Foundation Models for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12454)
*Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Roland Kwitt,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究将单变量时序基础模型TimesFM零样本应用于多变量时序异常检测（MTSAD），发现其无法有效检测持续异常，但能可靠检测分布变化（即变点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MTSAD模型依赖特定应用场景训练，成本高且难以扩展。基础模型（FMs）在单变量时序预测中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力，研究者希望探究其是否能在无需任务特定训练的情况下解决MTSAD问题。

Method: 将TimesFM作为零样本模型应用于SWaT工业基准数据集，采用两种策略：（1）逐特征预测器：基于阈值化预测误差进行异常检测；（2）嵌入器：利用中间层表示输入标准离群检测器。并与已建立基线方法进行对比。

Result: 两种零样本策略均未达到有竞争力的基线性能。嵌入表示仅显示异常段与正常段的部分分离，不足以实现可靠检测。原因在于FM过于有效地捕获时序动态，即使在完全异常的窗口中也产生低预测误差，导致持续异常与正常行为难以区分。然而，误差在异常边界处出现峰值，表明FM能可靠检测分布变化。

Conclusion: 提出的朴素零样本FM不适用于MTSAD，但在变点检测任务上具有潜力。

Abstract: Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection (MTSAD) is essential for reliability and safety in domains such as industrial process monitoring and financial risk management, yet conventional approaches rely on application-specific models that are costly to train and hard to scale. Foundation Models (FMs), pre-trained on broad data with strong zero-shot generalization, have recently become available for univariate time series forecasting, raising the question of whether they can address MTSAD without task-specific training. We investigate the zero-shot application of a univariate forecasting FM, TimesFM, to industrial MTSAD on the Secure Water Treatment (SWaT) benchmark, evaluating two strategies: treating the FM as a per-feature forecaster with thresholded prediction errors, and as an embedder whose intermediate representations feed standard outlier detectors. Neither of our proposed setups is competitive with established baselines; embeddings reveal only partial separation between normal and anomalous segments, insufficient for reliable detection. The cause is that the FM is too effective at capturing temporal dynamics, yielding low error even within fully anomalous windows, so persistent anomalies become indistinguishable from normal behavior. However, these observations yield valuable insights: the error peaks at anomaly boundaries, indicating FMs reliably detect distribution changes. We conclude that the proposed naive zero-shot FMs are unsuitable for MTSAD but promising for change-point detection.

</details>


### [199] [A JoLT for the KV Cache: Near-Lossless KV Cache Compression via Joint Tucker and JL-Residual Allocation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12550)
*Rahul Krishnan,Volker Schulz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: JoLT提出了一种基于三阶张量视角的KV缓存压缩方法，使用部分Tucker分解压缩token和特征维度，结合JL旋转低比特残差，在1字节预算下实现近无损2-3x压缩，在Mistral-7B和LLaMA-2-13B上困惑度、GSM8K准确率和RULER检索均无显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存随batch size、上下文长度和深度增长，已成为Transformer推理的主要内存瓶颈，尤其是在长上下文场景下，KV缓存而非模型权重决定了吞吐量上限。现有低秩方法和量化方法均未利用缓存作为三阶张量（head、token、feature三个轴冗余度不同）的自然结构。

Method: 将每层KV缓存视为三阶张量（head×token×feature），采用部分Tucker分解仅压缩token和feature轴，保留head和layer轴；使用Johnson-Lindenstrauss旋转的低比特残差恢复截断丢弃的能量；通过单个Lagrangian对偶在每层组内联合分配Tucker秩和残差位宽（对K和V分别处理），总预算为1字节。

Result: 在Mistral-7B-v0.3（GQA）和LLaMA-2-13B（MHA）上实现2-3x近无损压缩：困惑度、GSM8K准确率和RULER检索均保持在基线统计噪声范围内。2x压缩时，相对Frobenius误差K为0.009、V为0.006，比跨层SVD和4-bit量化低约一个数量级。随机SVD变体FlashJoLT在同等质量下实现5-13x压缩加速。

Conclusion: 利用KV缓存的三阶张量结构，通过部分Tucker分解+JL旋转残差的混合方法，可以在极低误差下实现显著压缩，优于纯低秩或纯量化方法。该方法同时适用于GQA和MHA架构。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become the dominant memory cost of transformer inference. It grows with batch size, context length, and depth, and at long context it, rather than the model weights, sets the ceiling on throughput. Two families of methods reduce it. Low-rank methods factor two-dimensional slices of the cache, either per-head matrices or cross-layer feature blocks, and quantization methods lower the bit-width of every entry. Neither family exploits the fact that the cache at a layer is naturally a third-order tensor whose three axes, the heads, the tokens, and the features, carry very different amounts of redundancy. We take this tensor view directly. Our method, JoLT, applies a partial Tucker decomposition that compresses only the token and feature axes while leaving the head and layer axes intact, and then restores the energy that truncation discards with a Johnson-Lindenstrauss (JL) rotated low-bit residual. A single Lagrangian dual allocates the Tucker ranks and the residual bit-widths together, per layer group and separately for keys and values, under one byte budget. The result is a near-lossless 2-3x compression: perplexity, GSM8K accuracy, and RULER needle-in-a-haystack retrieval all stay at or within statistical noise of the uncompressed baseline on both a grouped-query-attention model (Mistral-7B-v0.3) and a multi-head-attention model (LLaMA-2-13B). At 2x, JoLT reconstructs the cache to relative Frobenius error 0.009 (K) and 0.006 (V) on both architectures, roughly an order of magnitude below cross-layer SVD and 4-bit quantization. A randomized-SVD variant, FlashJoLT, delivers a 5-13x compression-time speedup at matched quality.

</details>


### [200] [Sample Efficient Generative Optimization for Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12488)
*Sarina Kopf,Cristina Nevado,Philippe Schwaller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SEGO是一个样本高效的分子优化框架，结合贝叶斯优化与生成模型，在仅需其他方法十分之一评估次数的情况下达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化面临化学空间巨大且评估预算有限的挑战，现有方法样本效率不足，难以在少量实验或高保真度评估下找到优秀候选分子。

Method: SEGO采用概率代理模型（高斯过程等）对化学空间中高奖励区域进行建模，引导生成模型在该区域生成候选分子，通过采集函数选择最有希望的候选，并用真实奖励更新代理模型和生成器。

Result: 在PMO基准上，使用其他方法十分之一的oracle调用达到SOTA性能；在多参数对接任务中，用约一半的oracle调用即找到10个命中分子。

Conclusion: SEGO显著提升了分子优化的样本效率，使直接由实验反馈驱动的优化策略更加可行。

Abstract: Molecular optimization in drug discovery, materials design, and catalysis requires searching vast chemical spaces under tight evaluation budgets, since high-fidelity oracles and experimental measurements are costly. The practical impact of an optimization method therefore hinges on its sample efficiency: how few evaluations it needs to find strong candidates. We introduce Sample Efficient Generative Optimization (SEGO), a framework for Bayesian optimization on adaptively generated molecules. In SEGO, a probabilistic surrogate model forms a hypothesis about where hits lie in chemical space, a generative model is steered to propose candidates in that region, the most promising candidate is selected via an acquisition function, and the resulting oracle call is used both to sharpen the surrogate and to anchor the generator in real reward. SEGO attains state-of-the-art performance on the practical molecular optimization (PMO) benchmark using only one tenth of the oracle calls consumed by other methods, and on a multiparameter docking task it reaches ten hits in roughly half the oracle calls of existing approaches. These gains move molecular optimization closer to campaigns driven by direct experimental feedback.

</details>


### [201] [Adversarial Attacks on Online Handwriting using Salience-based Temporal Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12500)
*Yataro Tamura,Brian Kenji Iwana,Jiseok Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于显著性引导的时间编辑对抗攻击方法，通过在线手写轨迹的时间序列上插入和删除点来生成对抗样本，而非添加加性噪声，从而保持手写形状和流畅性。实验表明该方法在单次黑盒迁移攻击中表现优于传统图像攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法主要针对图像输入，依赖加性空间扰动，当应用于在线手写（时间序列）时会产生高频抖动和明显不自然的笔画伪影。

Method: 提出基于显著性引导的时间编辑对抗攻击框架，通过梯度激活映射估计时间显著性，在显著性高的时间步上插入或删除点，生成对抗样本。

Result: 在Unipen和CASIA-OLHWDB数据集上，传统图像攻击在白盒设置下表现强但迁移性差，而时间编辑攻击在单次黑盒迁移设置下表现更强且保持手写视觉结构。

Conclusion: 时间编辑攻击对在线手写识别构成实际威胁，尤其在单次黑盒迁移场景下。

Abstract: Deep learning models for online handwriting recognition have been shown effective and are increasingly deployed in practical applications. However, their vulnerability to adversarial attacks is still a challenge. Existing adversarial methods are predominantly designed for image-based inputs and typically rely on additive spatial perturbations. When applied to online handwriting, which is inherently represented as a time series of pen trajectories, such perturbations often introduce high-frequency jitter and visibly unnatural stroke artifacts. In this work, we propose a novel adversarial attack framework for online handwriting recognition based on salience-guided temporal editing. Instead of adding noise, the proposed method generates adversarial examples by inserting and deleting points at time steps selected according to temporal salience, preserving the shape and smoothness of the original handwriting. Temporal salience is estimated using gradient-based activation mapping, which guides edits toward time steps that strongly support the original class prediction. We evaluate the proposed approach on the Unipen and CASIA-OLHWDB datasets under both white-box and one-shot black-box attack settings. Experimental results demonstrate that while conventional image-based attacks achieve strong white-box performance, they exhibit poor transferability across models. In contrast, the proposed temporal editing attack achieves stronger one-shot black-box transferability while preserving the visual structure of the handwriting. These results indicate that temporal editing is a relevant threat model for online handwriting recognition, particularly in one-shot black-box transfer settings.

</details>


### [202] [Extractable Memorization From First Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12649)
*A. Feder Cooper,Marika Swanberg,Jamie Hayes,Lea Duesterwald,Christopher De Sa,Daniel E. Ho,Mark A. Lemley,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM可提取记忆验证中的效度问题进行形式化分析，提出匹配比较方法（共形检验和普查），以区分记忆与可预测性，并重新定义“可提取记忆”需同时满足有效记忆主张和现实预算内高概率生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可提取记忆研究面临两方面的效度问题：一是过度提取（序列过短，无法区分记忆与可预测性），二是低估提取（模型也可能生成未训练过的真实文本）。两者都忽视了有效提取主张的关键：模型必须对训练序列表现出足够高的生成概率，以指示记忆而非可预测性。

Method: 提出匹配比较形式化框架，包含两种方法：(1) 共形检验——在训练序列和非训练序列来自总体时，校准指定假阳性率下的阈值；(2) 普查——当对象是单个文档（如书籍）时，通过与匹配的非训练文档对比校准。通过比较训练序列与可比非训练序列的生成概率基线，判断训练序列是否超出基线以证明记忆。

Result: 匹配比较能够实现严格、校准的记忆主张。实证发现：在Wikipedia上，OLMo 2 32B生成非训练10-token后缀的频率约为训练后缀的24%，这部分不是记忆而是假阳性；对于Llama 3.1 70B在书籍上的实验，校准阈值低至1e-27，支持某些序列的记忆主张，尽管实际采样预算几乎不可能提取。

Conclusion: 基于以上结果，作者对“可提取记忆”概念进行精炼，要求同时满足有效的记忆主张（通过匹配比较验证）和现实预算内近乎确定的生成能力。

Abstract: Recent work on extractable memorization in LLMs suffers from two contrasting validity problems. Some studies overstate extraction, e.g., relying on sequences too short to distinguish memorization from predictability. Others imply that extraction is unreliable evidence of memorization, since models can also reproduce real-world text they weren't explicitly trained on. In different ways, both overlook what makes a valid extraction claim: the model must generate a training sequence with high enough probability to indicate memorization. To determine what's high enough, one has to perform a matched comparison: measuring the generation probabilities of both the training sequences of interest and comparable non-training sequences. Because non-training sequences cannot have been memorized, their probabilities provide a baseline for predictability; a training sequence exceeding this baseline provides evidence of memorization. We formalize matched comparisons in two ways: (1) a conformal test that calibrates a threshold to a chosen FPR when training and non-training sequences are sampled from populations, and (2) a census that calibrates against a matched non-training document when the object is a single document (e.g., a book). We show that matched comparisons enable rigorous, calibrated memorization claims, and reveal where prior setups have validity issues. For instance, on Wikipedia OLMo 2 32B reproduces non-training 10-token suffixes roughly 24% as often as training ones: that share of the training generation rate reflects false positives, not memorization. For Llama 3.1 70B on books, the thresholds we calibrate are as low as 1e-27, supporting memorization claims for sequences that no feasible sampling budget would extract. Based on these results, we refine "extractable memorization" to require a valid memorization claim and near-certain generation within a realistic budget.

</details>


### [203] [What Does Goodness Measure? A Likelihood-Ratio Account of Forward-Forward Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12501)
*Paolo Giannitrapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从显式生成模型视角重新解释了Forward-Forward算法的两个核心启发式设计：平方 goodness 实质是零均值异尺度总体下似然比检验的充分统计量与决策边界；层间归一化需保留坐标能量而去除长度，否则会导致深度塌缩；成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化 goodness 可消除该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Forward-Forward算法中平方 goodness 和层间归一化通常被视为启发式设计，缺乏理论依据。本文旨在从显式生成模型视角为这些选择提供严格的统计解释。

Method: 将平方 goodness 重新解释为两个零均值但尺度不同的总体间似然比检验的充分统计量；将FF阈值视为决策边界。对于各向异性总体推广到马氏距离 goodness；对于重尾总体推导出饱和统计量（后验精度/除性归一化）。分析层间归一化的必要条件是去除长度而保留每坐标能量，并通过实验展示单位范数归一化导致的深度塌缩现象。

Result: 平方 goodness 是各向同性零均值异尺度总体的似然比检验充分统计量；各向异性情况需马氏距离 goodness；重尾分布下统计量饱和且斜率等于后验精度（除性归一化），证据有界且仅在聚合时受益；层间归一化必须去除长度而非单位范数，否则出现深度塌缩；成对目标存在尺度膨胀捷径，白化 goodness 可移除该捷径。

Conclusion: Forward-Forward算法的关键设计选择（平方 goodness、层间归一化）并非任意启发式，而是可以从显式生成模型的统计推断原理中推导出来，为算法的理解和改进提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The Forward-Forward (FF) algorithm trains each layer locally, so that a scalar goodness - the sum of squared activations - is high on real inputs and low on contrastive ones, with activations normalized between layers. Both choices are usually treated as heuristics. Under an explicit generative model they are not: the squared goodness is the sufficient statistic of a likelihood-ratio test between two zero-mean populations differing in scale, and the FF threshold is its boundary. It generalizes: anisotropic populations yield a Mahalanobis goodness, the plain square being its isotropic case; heavy-tailed populations yield a saturating statistic whose slope is a posterior precision - divisive normalization - with bounded evidence and an advantage only under aggregation. The same lens characterizes the inter-layer normalization: it must remove the length while preserving per-coordinate energy, explaining a depth collapse we observe under unit-norm normalization; and the pairwise objective admits a scale-inflation shortcut that a whitened goodness removes.

</details>


### [204] [OOD-RL-Bench: A Benchmark Framework for Out-of-Distribution Detection in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12523)
*Emil Mittag,Richard Dazeley,Peter Vamplew*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OOD-RL-Bench，一个用于评估强化学习轨迹中离分布检测器的标准化框架，实验表明观测扰动和状态切换容易被检测，而观测延迟和动作条件动态仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前OOD检测基准仅评估图像分类器或静态低维数据集，未能捕捉强化学习轨迹中复杂的、动作依赖的时间结构，缺乏针对RL领域OOD检测器的标准化评估框架。

Method: 构建OOD-RL-Bench框架，通过共享接口和配置集成检测器与异常注入器，支持无需修改核心基准循环即可评估新评分方法和扰动家族；使用DQN策略在LunarLander-v3环境中评估多种异常类型。

Result: 观测扰动和状态切换可被多种方法高精度识别；观测延迟和动作条件动态即使在同时间步比较异常分数与正常分数时仍难以检测；不同异常类型间检测性能差异显著。

Conclusion: OOD-RL-Bench为RL领域的OOD检测提供了可复现的标准化评估工具，揭示了现有检测器在不同异常类型上的能力边界，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Reliable reinforcement learning (RL) agents must maintain operational integrity amidst sensor malfunctions, dynamic disturbances, and slow environmental shifts. The detection of out-of-distribution conditions is pivotal to determining when an agent's observations, transitions, or trajectory dynamics deviate from the assumptions underpinning its policy training. Current out-of-distribution (OOD) detection benchmarks typically evaluate image classifiers or static low-dimensional datasets, failing to account for the complex, action-dependent temporal structure inherent in RL trajectories. To address this gap, we present OOD-RL-Bench, a comprehensive and extensible framework designed to evaluate OOD detectors against categories of anomalies injected into RL trajectories. Detectors and anomaly injectors are integrated through shared interfaces and configuration, which allows new scoring methods and perturbation families to be evaluated without modification of the core benchmark loop. We evaluate the utility of the framework using a Deep Q-Network policy within the LunarLander-v3 environment. We assess the performance of each detector across a suite of anomaly types using matched-time AUROC, matched-time AUPRC, matched-time false-positive rate, detection delay, and segmented-onset metrics. Our analysis reveals significant performance variance across anomaly types: observation perturbations and regime switches are identified with high accuracy by several methods, while observation delay and action-conditioned dynamics remain difficult even when post-onset anomaly scores are compared against clean scores from the same timesteps. We make the framework, trained policy checkpoint, and complete results publicly available as a reproducible artefact.

</details>


### [205] [Learning Mechanistic Reasoning for Chemical Reactions with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12771)
*Xingyu Dang,Haocheng Tang,Junmei Wang,Yanjun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模反应机理推理数据集，并基于Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism建立了FukuyamaBench基准，通过微调Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在机理路径精确匹配上达到8.3%，超越专业FlowER模型（5.1%），证明机理感知训练能显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前化学大语言模型主要聚焦粗粒度的命名反应进行产物预测和逆合成分析，常导致物理不一致性和幻觉；而专门用于机理推断的小规模生成模型在不同化学空间中的泛化能力受限。为克服这些局限，需要构建大规模反应机理推理数据集并建立相应基准。

Method: 构建大规模反应机理推理数据集，并建立FukuyamaBench基准（来自Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism），对Qwen3-30B-A3B模型进行微调。

Result: 微调后的Qwen3-30B-A3B在FukuyamaBench Set A上实现8.3%的精确路径匹配，超越专用FlowER模型（5.1%）。

Conclusion: 机理感知训练能显著增强语言模型的化学推理能力，且中等规模推理模型通过适当训练可在机理推断任务上超越专用小模型。

Abstract: Reaction mechanisms consist of the step-by-step sequences of elementary reactions that explain chemical transformations. Learning the mechanism logic is therefore essential for enhancing the fundamental chemical intelligence of large language models (LLMs). The stepwise deduction of reaction mechanism aligns naturally with the reasoning paradigms of reasoning LLMs. However, current chemical LLMs primarily emphasize coarse-grained name reactions for product prediction and retrosynthesis, often leading to physical inconsistencies and hallucinations. In contrast, specialized small-scale generative models for mechanism inference typically suffer from restricted generalization capacity across diverse chemical spaces. To overcome these limitations, we built a novel, large-scale reasoning dataset of reaction mechanisms. Furthermore, we established the FukuyamaBench, a difficult benchmark derived from Fukuyama's Advanced Organic Reaction Mechanism book, to rigorously evaluate model performance on hierarchical mechanism reasoning. Our fine-tuned Qwen3-30B-A3B achieves 8.3% exact pathway match on FukuyamaBench Set~A, surpassing the specialized FlowER model (5.1%), demonstrating that mechanism-aware training substantially enhances chemical reasoning in language models.

</details>


### [206] [From Preimage Search To Source-Grounded Feature Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12526)
*Kaixiang Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出源锚定特征反演方法，通过局部修复反向传播信号，无需逐查询优化即可将神经网络内部特征可视化到输入空间，并验证反演结果依赖于源样本而非目标无关模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征反演方法仅匹配目标特征，忽略了生成该特征的源样本信息，导致反演结果不唯一且缺乏可解释性。

Method: 利用反向传播提供的正确反向依赖，在计算图边界通过闭式矩阵维纳映射修复反向信号：第一个维纳映射从均值种子VJP估计上游状态，第二个维纳映射校正JVP前向一致性残差，将修复后的状态沿同一计算图反向传播。

Result: 该方法支持CNN和Transformer架构、不同张量分量和视觉分布，无需查询特定优化；通过匹配目标和源控制实验验证反演依赖所选特征和源样本局部算子，而非目标无关模板。

Conclusion: 源锚定特征反演实现了对隐藏特征层次在单层和单通道级别的检查，将网络从输入中提取的特征与塑造决策的内部证据联系起来。

Abstract: Interpreting a neural network requires understanding what its internal features extract from a particular input. Feature inversion seeks to express a selected feature in the input domain, but canonical iterative methods search for an input whose re-encoded representation matches the target. Because many inputs can satisfy this constraint, target matching alone does not specify the inverse associated with the sample that generated the feature. We formulate source-grounded feature inversion by conditioning the inverse on the source-local network geometry at the target-generating input. At each boundary of the computational DAG, backpropagation provides the correct reverse dependencies but transports an adjoint signal rather than an upstream-state estimate. We locally repair this signal with a closed-form matrix Wiener map from a mean-seed VJP to the upstream state, followed by a second Wiener map for the JVP forward-consistency residual, and compose the repaired states through the same DAG in one finite reverse pass. One calibrated zero-intercept map family supports new inputs, depths, channels, and channel groups across diverse CNN and Transformer architectures, tensor components, and visual distributions without query-specific optimisation. Matched target and source controls verify that each inverse depends on the selected feature and the local operators of the sample being explained, rather than a target-independent image template. Prediction-conditioned feature atlases align these visualisations with independent interventions on the corresponding internal features. Together, source-grounded feature inversion opens the model's hidden feature hierarchy to inspection at the level of individual layers and channels, linking what the network extracts from an input to the internal evidence that shapes its decision.

</details>


### [207] [Lightweight Multi-Scale Anomaly Detection for Resource-Constrained Edge Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12599)
*Raheen Junaid Wani,Smruti R. Sarangi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级多尺度自编码器（LMSAE），用于单变量时间序列异常检测。该模型利用离散小波变换提取多尺度特征，并采用多尺度损失函数增强对细微异常的敏感性。在基准数据集上，LMSAE以小于500KB的模型尺寸和显著更少的参数实现了竞争甚至更优的检测性能。在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上，推理延迟降低9倍，功耗降低2倍，适合边缘部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列异常检测方法在计算开销、模型大小和功耗方面难以满足IoT、传感器网络和边缘监控等场景的严格约束，且多尺度敏感性不足，难以捕捉细微异常。

Method: 提出LMSAE网络，采用离散小波变换（DWT）提取多尺度特征，并设计多尺度损失函数以增强对细微或隐藏异常的敏感性。整体架构紧凑高效，模型尺寸小于500KB。

Result: 在基准数据集上，以更少的参数和小于500KB的模型尺寸取得了竞争甚至更优的检测性能。在NVIDIA Jetson Nano上，推理延迟降低9倍，功耗降低2倍。

Conclusion: LMSAE是一种适合边缘部署的轻量级时间序列异常检测方案，在保持高检测性能的同时显著降低了计算资源需求。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is increasingly important in IoT systems, sensor networks, and edge monitoring applications, where models must operate under strict constraints on memory, latency, and power consumption. While recent deep-learning approaches have improved detection accuracy, many remain computationally expensive and often fail to capture subtle anomalies due to limited multi-scale sensitivity. Autoencoders are widely used for anomaly detection because they reconstruct normal patterns well, leading to elevated reconstruction errors for anomalous inputs. Their simplicity and efficiency also make them suitable lightweight backbones for handling multi-scale inputs. To address these challenges, we propose a Lightweight MultiScale AutoEncoder (LMSAE) network for univariate time-series anomaly detection, designed to be compact and computationally efficient. LMSAE leverages the Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT) to extract multi-scale features and employs a multi-scale loss function to improve sensitivity to subtle or hidden anomalies. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate competitive or superior detection performance despite using significantly fewer parameters and a model size of less than 500 KB. LMSAE also achieves low-latency, low-power inference on the NVIDIA Jetson Nano, with 9x reduction in inference latency and 2x reduction in power consumption, making it ideal for edge deployment.

</details>


### [208] [Accelerating Masked Diffusion Large Language Models: A Survey of Efficient Inference Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12829)
*Daehoon Gwak,Minhyung Lee,Junwoo Park,Jaegul Choo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的延迟分解框架，用于分析扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的推理效率，并从算法、架构/系统以及推理时扩展三个维度系统综述了加速技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型在并行生成上具有理论优势，但并行生成本身并不能保证实际加速。推理效率成为实际部署的前提条件，然而端到端延迟受算法、架构和系统层面因素的复杂权衡影响，现有基准测试常混淆这些因素，导致严格比较困难。

Method: 提出统一的延迟分解框架来解耦dLLM推理中的算法、架构和系统因素；基于此框架，将加速技术分为三类：算法创新、架构与系统优化、推理时扩展。

Result: 给出了一个用于dLLM推理延迟的系统化分析框架，并据此对现有加速技术进行了分类和梳理。

Conclusion: 提供了可复现基准测试的指导原则，并指出了实现并行生成潜力所面临的开源挑战。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a theoretical advantage in parallel generation over standard autoregressive models. However, parallel generation alone does not guarantee practical speedups. Realizing this efficiency requires specialized inference mechanisms, such as diffusion-aware caching and reuse. Consequently, as inference efficiency becomes a prerequisite for practical deployment, recent research has actively explored acceleration techniques across algorithms, architectures, and systems. However, rigorous comparisons remain difficult, as end-to-end latency stems from intricate trade-offs between algorithmic, architectural, and system-level factors that are often conflated in existing benchmarks. In this survey, we introduce a unified latency decomposition framework for dLLMs to disentangle these factors and analyze their impact on inference speed in real deployments. Guided by this framework, we categorize acceleration techniques along three axes covering algorithmic innovations, architectural and system optimizations, and inference-time scaling. Finally, we provide guidelines for reproducible benchmarking and highlight open challenges for realizing the full potential of parallel generation.

</details>


### [209] [The Geometry of Memorization: Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity as a Diagnostic for Flow Matching Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12616)
*Shuchan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限时间谱灵敏度(FTSS)指标g(t)，通过跟踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值揭示连续时间生成框架中的流形几何结构，并发现过拟合会导致谱坍塌，从而利用内部轨迹动力学检测生成记忆化，无需外部查询或基线数据比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续时间生成框架中，理论目标偏好直线轨迹，但实际网络会发展出复杂的路径形变。现有方法缺乏对流动几何结构的理解，且检测生成记忆化通常需要外部成员查询或基线数据比较，成本高昂。

Method: 提出FTSS g(t)指标，这是一个无梯度、前向传播的度量，通过跟踪状态转移矩阵的均方根奇异值来暴露流动几何结构，作为稳定秩的连续代理。利用g(t)在数据稀缺场景下的行为差异（泛化模型保持稳定有效维度，过拟合导致谱坍塌）进行内部几何审计。

Result: g(t)揭示了一种数据稀缺下的几何病理：泛化模型保持稳定有效维度，而过拟合导致谱坍塌。基于此现象的框架能够利用纯内部轨迹动力学检测生成记忆化。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了无需外部成员查询或基线数据比较的生成记忆化检测，为连续时间生成模型提供了一种新的内部几何审计工具。

Abstract: Continuous-time generative frameworks construct probability paths between base and target domains by optimizing time-dependent velocity fields. While theoretical targets favor straight trajectories, empirical networks develop complex path deformations. This paper presents the Finite-Time Spectral Sensitivity (FTSS) g(t), a gradient-free, forward-pass metric that exposes flow geometry by tracking the root-mean-square singular value of the state-transition matrix. Serving as a continuous proxy for stable rank, g(t) reveals a distinct geometric pathology under data scarcity: while generalizing models maintain stable effective dimensions, overfitting causes a spectral collapse. We leverage this structural phenomenon to develop an internal geometric audit based on g(t). Our framework detects generative memorization using purely internal trajectory dynamics, removing the need for external membership queries or baseline data comparison.

</details>


### [210] [Learning Forced Multibody Dynamics on Lie Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12627)
*Martine Dyring Hansen,Marta Ghirardelli,Elena Celledoni,David Martin de Diego,Brynjulf Owren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程在流形上的架构，仅利用位置数据学习机械系统的动力学，该方法能够保持系统的几何结构并适用于多体系统和有外部控制输入的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机械系统动力学学习往往需要完整的状态信息（包括速度数据），但在实际应用中速度测量可能不可用或含有噪声，且忽略系统几何结构会导致物理不一致的预测。因此需要一种仅依赖位置数据、同时保持系统几何不变性和守恒律的学习方法。

Method: 基于离散受迫欧拉-拉格朗日方程在Lie群上的数值积分格式，将力学系统的状态演化建模在流形值位形空间上，直接使用位置观测数据进行学习，并通过几何结构约束保证物理一致性。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实世界数据集上均表现出强性能，能够有效应用于多体系统并处理外部控制输入。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何感知学习方法仅需位置数据即可准确学习机械系统动力学，同时保持系统的几何不变性和守恒律，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: We propose an architecture for learning the dynamics of mechanical systems based on discrete forced Euler-Lagrange equations on Lie groups using only position data.
