This adaptation requires two complementary behaviors: deformation, where the team continuously reshapes its geometry while remaining connected, and reconfiguration, where robots split into subgroups or merge back into a single formation. Existing methods often model these behaviors independently, connect them through handcrafted rules, or lack explicit geometric criteria for determining when each behavior should be invoked. However, challenging environments may require online changes in formation shape, connectivity, and effective team composition, making decoupled or rule-based approaches prone to suboptimal trajectories and deadlock. We propose EFLUX, a geometry-grounded LLM agentic framework for automatic and elastic multi-robot formation navigation. EFLUX extracts a structured scene representation and uses an LLM to reason jointly over both deformation actions, such as scaling and shearing, and reconfiguration actions, such as splitting and merging. These strategies are then translated into executable per-robot waypoints through a closed-loop generation, verification, and correction pipeline. Simulation and hardware experiments show that EFLUX enables safe, continuous, and elastic formation navigation in constrained environments, reducing deadlock and navigation failures compared with baselines while maintaining coherent multi-robot coordination.

</details>


### [278] [Enabling 24-hour Agricultural Robotics: Unsupervised Day-to-Night Cross-Modal Image Translation for Nighttime Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12065)
*Robel Mamo,Rajitha de Silva,Grzegorz Cielniak,Taeyeong Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出无监督图像翻译框架，将白天的农业RGB图像转换为近红外夜间图像，无需像素级标注即可训练夜间感知模型，实验证明在夜间农业视觉导航中有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有农业视觉导航系统主要针对白天设计，夜间部署具有全天候监测等优势，但缺乏大规模标注的夜间图像数据集，限制了夜间感知模型的训练。

Method: 提出基于CLIP的无监督图像翻译框架（保持语义一致性），引入可见性掩码处理近红外照明有效范围有限的问题，使用白天语义标签直接训练夜间分割模型。

Result: 与SOTA图像翻译基线相比，生成图像质量更高；下游夜间语义分割任务性能提升；发布首个夜间农业视觉导航基准数据集AgriNight（428张白天+549张夜间图像），并在真实机器人上完成夜间自主导航实验。

Conclusion: 无监督白天到近红外夜间图像翻译方法可行且有效，CLIP模型有助于保持语义一致性，可见性掩码改善了夜间场景建模，为农业机器人全天候视觉导航提供了新方案。

Abstract: While visual navigation has been extensively studied in agricultural robotics, most existing systems assume daytime conditions. In fact, deploying autonomous robots at night offers significant advantages, including 24-hour crop and soil monitoring, fruit harvesting, and nocturnal pest detection. Modern vision-based systems, however, rely heavily on large-scale well-annotated image datasets, which remains challenging to obtain for nighttime operation scenarios. To address this, we propose an unsupervised image translation framework that converts daytime plant-row RGB images into near-infrared (NIR) nighttime counterparts without requiring pixel-to-pixel supervision. This enables the direct reuse of daytime semantic labels for training nighttime perception models. In particular, by incorporating a pre-trained Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training (CLIP) model, the proposed framework is designed to preserve semantic consistency during day-to-night translation. Additionally, a visibility mask is introduced to account for the limited effective range of NIR illumination in nighttime scenes. We conduct comparative evaluations with state-of-the-art image translation baselines and demonstrate higher image qualities, as supported by improved performance in downstream semantic segmentation for nighttime visual navigation. For evaluation, we utilize AgriNight--a novel dataset comprising 428 daytime and 549 nighttime images collected using night-vision-equipped mobile robots in agricultural fields and manually annotated with pixel-wise semantic labels--and introduce it as the first benchmark for nighttime agricultural visual navigation. We also perform real-time autonomous navigation experiments with a physical robot operating at night. The data and code are available at: https://github.com/mamorobel/AgriNight.

</details>


### [279] [Robust In-Hand Manipulation via Priors in Reinforcement Learning and Mechanical Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12105)
*Yifei Chen,Shihan Lu,Ed Colgate,Kevin Lynch*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种互补的物理先验（全局抓取质量先验和局部接触几何先验）来增强机器人手指滚动操作的学习，实验表明该方法显著提升了旋转效率、抓取稳定性和抗干扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无外部传感的指尖操作因指-物接触的不确定性和重力干扰而具有挑战性，现有强化学习方法不注重维持良好条件的抓取以支持持续操作。

Method: 提出两种物理先验：1）全局抓取质量先验（基于经典抓取分析，作为密集奖励整形项）；2）局部接触几何先验（基于指尖曲率，从机械层面塑造接触界面使其朝向任务对齐的滚动）。

Result: 在多指机械手对三种物体、四种手掌方向的操作实验中，旋转效率、抓取稳定性和抗干扰能力均显著提升。

Conclusion: 嵌入在学习和指尖形态学中的物理先验能够提高任务鲁棒性和sim-to-real迁移能力。

Abstract: In-hand manipulation without external sensing is challenging due to uncertainties from finger-object contacts and disturbances by gravity. While reinforcement learning has shown promise in learning complex finger gaiting, existing approaches do not prioritize maintaining well-conditioned grasps for sustained manipulation. We introduce two complementary physics priors for robust in-hand rolling: a global grasp-quality prior derived from classical grasp analysis and a local contact-geometry prior based on fingertip curvature. The grasp-quality prior is used as a dense reward-shaping term that encourages well-distributed contacts with improved worst-case wrench resistance. The contact-geometry prior is expressed in the fingertip geometry that mechanically shapes the contact interface toward task-aligned rolling while reducing off-axis drift. We evaluate the effect of these priors on learning in-hand rolling manipulation for a multifingered robotic hand manipulating three different objects at four palm orientations. Results show significant improvement in rotation efficiency, grasp stability, and disturbance rejection, suggesting that physics priors embedded in both learning and fingertip morphology improve task robustness and sim-to-real transfer. An overview video can be found at https://youtu.be/pdd1wHxQnJM?si=dM-U5kiiPTYsk3Pk.

</details>


### [280] [GaitSpan: Growing Humanoid Locomotion from Walking to Running](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12114)
*Kwan-Yee Lin,Zilin Wang,Janelle J. Liu,Stella X. Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GaitSpan是一个从基础行走策略扩展到更快运动（慢跑、奔跑）的框架，通过节奏生成、步幅塑造和残差适应三个机制，实现了单一策略下连续速度控制的类人运动，并跨形态、跨地形零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法获取步态多样性需要预设步态调度、模仿运动片段、训练专家策略切换或知识蒸馏，缺乏对连续速度指令、不同地形和机器人形态的灵活性。希望让已能行走的人形机器人无需从头学习就能自然过渡到慢跑或奔跑。

Method: GaitSpan将行走作为种子技能，通过三方面扩展：1) 节奏生成——冻结行走策略并注入多个内部时钟，学习基于指令的组合规范动作；2) 步幅塑造——基于弹簧-负载倒立摆动力学的物理目标奖励高速指令下的动态步态模式；3) 残差适应——捕获前两者未覆盖的运动细节。

Result: 首次实现单一指令条件化的人形策略覆盖从行走到慢跑到奔跑的连续速度范围，跨形态迁移，在未见过的仿真到仿真和真实世界地形上零样本部署，相比多专家训练或人类模仿基线，学习更快且步态性能更强。

Conclusion: GaitSpan证明了从基础行走策略技能增长到更高速运动是可行且高效的，为连续步态控制和跨形态迁移提供了新范式。

Abstract: A humanoid that can walk should not relearn locomotion from scratch to jog or run. Yet current approaches often obtain gait diversity by prescribing gait schedules, imitating motion clips, training experts to switch between or distilling skills into one policy. These strategies can produce impressive behaviors, but offer limited flexibility across continuous speed commands, terrains, and morphologies. We study skill growth with GaitSpan, a framework that expands a pretrained, basic walking policy into faster locomotion. It treats walking as a seed skill: reusable motor structure for balance, support, body coordination, and contact transition that can be regenerated at new rhythms, extended into longer/higher strides, and corrected by residual adaptation. This expansion has three aspects: 1) rhythm generation, which modulates the frozen walking policy with multiple internal clocks and learns command-conditioned combinations of the resulting canonical actions; 2) stride shaping, which rewards dynamic locomotion patterns appropriate for higher commanded speeds using a physically grounded objective inspired by spring-loaded inverted pendulum dynamics; and 3) residual adaptation, which captures motion details not accounted for by rhythm generation or stride shaping. GaitSpan is the first to deliver a single command-conditioned humanoid policy that spans walking, jogging, and running-like regimes covering a continuous speed range, transfers across morphologies, and deploys zero-shot on unseen sim-to-sim, and real-world terrains. Compared with baselines either trained with multi-experts or imitation from humans, it learns faster and achieves stronger gait performance.

</details>


### [281] [A Behavioral State Vocabulary in Sony ERS-111 R-CODE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12115)
*Christopher A. Tucker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对索尼ERS-111 AIBO机器人的R-CODE示例程序库进行了语料层面的状态图分析，揭示了表面多样化的行为例程实际上由一套紧凑的具身语法构成，包括初始化、感知、迭代动作、同步和恢复等核心控制原语，并论证了这种基于状态的抽象表示对于在资源受限的机器人系统上构建模块化行为例程具有实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多孤立地阅读机器人行为脚本，缺乏从语料层面对行为图结构进行系统性比较与分析的工作。作者试图识别出构成示例程序库的重复性控制词汇，以揭示表面多样化行为背后的共同结构化语法。

Method: 对索尼R-CODE示例库中的行为图进行语料级分析，跨脚本比较命名状态，识别重复出现的控制原语和结构模式，从而聚合出一个以初始化、感知、迭代动作、同步和恢复为核心的具身语法框架。

Result: 发现许多表面上不同的行为例程实际上由一组紧凑的、可复现的控制结构构建而成，形成了以初始化、感知、迭代动作、同步和恢复为中心的具身语法体系。

Conclusion: 这种基于状态的抽象表示适合作为中间表示层，用于在资源受限的原生机器人系统上构建新的封装式行为例程，尤其适用于需要确定性控制、直接硬件访问和模块化行为组合的场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a corpus-level analysis of generated behavior diagrams derived from Sony's R-CODE sample distribution for the ERS-111 AIBO. Rather than reading each script in isolation, the study compares named states across the corpus to identify the recurring control vocabulary that structures the sample set. The resulting aggregate shows that many superficially different routines are built from a compact embodied grammar centered on initialization, sensing, iterative action, synchronization, and recovery. In addition to historical analysis, the paper argues that this form of state-based abstraction is useful as an intermediate representation for constructing new encapsulated behavior routines, especially on constrained native robotic systems where deterministic control, direct hardware access, and modular behavioral composition remain important.

</details>


### [282] [More than a Manipulator: Planning Propellant-Free Attitude Maneuvers for Free-Floating Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12130)
*Harsh G. Bhundiya,Avi Soval,Keenan Albee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究利用机械臂运动实现航天器无推进剂姿态控制，通过轨迹优化解决关节与碰撞约束问题，证明机械臂可作为冗余或主用姿态控制系统，特别适用于在轨组装与制造场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统航天器姿态控制依赖动量交换装置或消耗推进剂的推力器，而机械臂通常被视为干扰源而非执行器。本文旨在开发机械臂作为无推进剂姿态控制执行器的潜力。

Method: 将机械臂运动控制问题表述为包含关节约束和碰撞避免约束的轨迹优化问题，采用内点法求解非线性规划，并通过动量/转矩包络与反作用轮阵列进行对比。

Result: 针对不同运动学复杂度和质量特性的航天器-机械臂系统，成功演示了复杂调姿和消旋轨迹；证实机械臂可提供与反作用轮阵列相当的姿态控制能力。

Conclusion: 机械臂可作为冗余甚至主用姿态控制系统，在抓取高相对质量比载荷的在轨组装与制造中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Spacecraft attitude control is traditionally achieved using momentum exchange devices or propellant-consuming thrusters. Meanwhile, a growing number of missions require robotic manipulators, which are typically treated as disturbance sources to be rejected rather than as actuators for spacecraft reorientation. This work investigates the use of manipulator motions for propellant-free attitude control by formulating a trajectory optimization problem with critical joint and collision avoidance constraints. Using an interior point solver for the resulting nonlinear program, complex slew and detumble trajectories are demonstrated for a range of spacecraft-manipulator systems with varying kinematic complexity and mass properties. The achievable control authority is compared directly with that of reaction wheel arrays via momentum and torque envelopes, demonstrating the potential for manipulators to serve as redundant or even primary attitude control systems. This work provides a framework for using manipulators as multipurpose attitude control actuators, with particularly promising applications in in-space assembly and manufacturing when grasping payloads with high relative mass fractions.

</details>


### [283] [Analysis of Mutual and Referential Human and Robot Gazes in a Collaborative Word Association Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12181)
*Jens V. Rüppel,Tim Schreiter,Andrey Rudenko,Achim J. Lilienthal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过NAO机器人参与的协作词语联想游戏，发现机器人注视方向不影响参与者对机器人提议单词的首次注视时间，但参与者表达确认请求时会更频繁地注视机器人。结果表明在认知要求高的任务中，语言方面的影响超越了机器人目光的参考作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数关于人机对话中目光注视的研究聚焦于面对面社交对话，而在需要高度集中注意力的任务导向交互中，机器人目光的作用尚不明确。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究在认知要求高的协作任务中机器人目光如何影响人类视觉注意力。

Method: 参与者与NAO机器人（作为具身、LLM驱动的对话伙伴）进行协作词语联想游戏。实验在两种条件下进行（分别实现机器人的相互注视和参考注视），使用眼动追踪眼镜记录参与者目光，通过目光坐标、语音片段、关键事件和兴趣区域进行分析。

Result: 1) 机器人目光方向不影响参与者对机器人提议单词的首次注视时间；2) 当参与者的对话包含确认请求时，他们注视机器人的频率高于不包含确认请求时；3) 游戏的高认知需求导致语言方面削弱了机器人参考性目光的效果。

Conclusion: 在认知要求高的协作任务中，言语交流的主导作用会削弱机器人目光的参考性影响。研究结果为设计需要协调和高效沟通的协作任务中的机器人目光及轮换行为提供了有价值的见解。

Abstract: Robot gaze is a major component of human-robot dialogue coordination. Most studies of gaze in human-robot dialogue focus on face-to-face social conversations, but little is known about gaze in demanding task-focused interactions. In this paper, we investigate how the gaze of a robot game partner affects human visual attention and if humans tend to direct confirmation-seeking gazes towards the robot. In our study, we let participants play a collaborative word association game with a NAO robot acting as an embodied, LLM-driven conversational partner. Our experiments are conducted under two conditions, which implement mutual and referential gazes of the robot respectively. We record participants' gaze using eye tracking glasses and analyze the interactions using gaze coordinates, speech segments, key events and areas of interests. We find that robot gaze orientation does not affect the time to first fixation on words the robot proposed. We also find that participants gaze more often at the robot when their dialogue line contains confirmation requests, compared to when it does not. Our results indicate (likely also due to the cognitively demanding nature of the game) that the verbal aspect of this task overshadows the effects of referential robot gaze. These findings offer valuable insights for designing and validating robot gaze and turn-taking behavior in collaborative tasks which require coordination and efficient communication.

</details>


### [284] [Contract-Grounded Behavior Tree Synthesis via Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12220)
*Jonathan Salfity,Robert Blake Anderson,Mitch Pryor*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于契约的机器人行为树合成架构，通过MCP服务器提供技能库、BT算子和组合模板，使非专家用户能用自然语言指令生成可部署的BT，无需了解机器人实现细节。在模拟和真实机器人上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based BT合成方法将接地（grounding）责任推给提示作者，导致在作者不了解机器人实际可执行技能、参数化方式或运行时软件约束时部署脆弱。需要一种方法让非专家用户无需了解机器人实现细节即可可靠地生成可执行的BT。

Method: 提出契约接地BT合成架构：编码代理通过查询机器人端MCP服务器获取显式契约（技能库、允许的BT算子、可选BT组合模板），然后合成BT，经运行时验证门校验后执行。支持非专家用户用自然语言下达命令。

Result: 在110个PyRoboSim模拟任务和14个Husarion Panther实体机器人任务上评估了两种LLM（Sonnet 4.6闭源模型和Gemma4:31b开源模型）。契约接地实现了近乎完美的BT验证和高任务成功率；BT组合模板显著提升了小模型在反应式控制流任务上的性能；架构可迁移到运行Nav2堆栈的物理硬件。

Conclusion: 契约接地使得非专家用户无需了解机器人实现细节即可通过自然语言可靠合成BT，该方法在模拟和真实机器人场景中均有效，并且BT组合模板有助于弥补小模型在反应式控制流方面的不足。

Abstract: Synthesizing deployable robot behavior trees (BTs) from natural language (NL) requires grounding to ensure every generated BT references only skills a robot can actually execute. Existing LLM-based BT synthesis approaches often place this grounding responsibility on the prompt author. This makes deployment brittle when the author does not know which skills the robot can execute, how those skills are parameterized, or how the robot runtime software constrains valid BT structure. This paper proposes a contract-grounded BT synthesis architecture in which a coding agent queries a robot-side Model Context Protocol (MCP) server to retrieve an explicit contract consisting of a skill library, permitted BT operators, and optional BT composition templates, before synthesizing a BT for validation and execution. In our framework, non-expert operators issue NL commands without knowledge of robot implementation details, while a robot runtime validation gate enforces correctness before execution. We evaluate two LLMs, a closed model (Sonnet 4.6) and a smaller open-source model (Gemma4:31b), across 110 simulated tasks in PyRoboSim and 14 tasks on a physical Husarion Panther robot. Results show that contract grounding enables near-perfect BT validation and high task success, that BT composition templates substantially recover success on reactive control-flow tasks for the smaller model, and that the architecture transfers to physical hardware running a Nav2 stack opaque to both operator and agent.

</details>


### [285] [DiffRadar: Differentiable Physics-Aware Radar SLAM with Gaussian Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12265)
*Gaurav Bagwe,Xiaoyong Yuan,Yongji Wu,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiffRadar 提出了一种基于可微、物理感知高斯场的实时雷达SLAM系统，通过将雷达观测建模为各向异性高斯原语并在信号域进行可微渲染，实现了雷达测量中的机器人位姿与场景结构的联合优化。在公开基准和针对性压力测试上，DiffRadar 在轨迹误差、地图一致性等方面均显著优于现有方法，并保持70 FPS的实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达SLAM系统主要依赖离散化雷达热图上的扫描匹配来估计运动，破坏了几何连续性且无法捕捉雷达关键感知特性，在退化或动态变化环境中常导致位姿估计不稳定和地图质量下降。

Method: 将场景表示为各向异性高斯原语，通过可微雷达前向模型在距离-方位角和多普勒-方位角空间中渲染雷达测量，实现机器人位姿与场景结构的联合优化。

Result: 在Radarize基准测试上轨迹误差大幅降低，尤其在特征贫乏的走廊运动中提升显著；地图一致性翻倍以上，同时保持70 FPS的实时性能。

Conclusion: 在信号域直接建模雷达观测能够显著提升雷达SLAM的鲁棒性和一致性，为移动平台的纯雷达 SLAM 提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Radar sensing is increasingly used in mobile systems because it operates reliably under poor lighting, adverse weather, and privacy-sensitive settings where cameras and LiDAR often fail. However, most existing radar SLAM systems estimate motion through scan matching on discretized radar heatmaps, which breaks geometric continuity and fails to capture key radar sensing properties, often leading to unstable pose estimation and degraded mapping in regenerate or dynamically changing environments. We present DiffRadar, a real-time radar SLAM system that models radar observations as a differentiable, physics-aware Gaussian field rather than discrete scans. DiffRadar represents the scene as anisotropic Gaussian primitives and renders radar measurements in range-azimuth and Doppler-azimuth spaces through a differentiable radar forward model, enabling joint optimization of robot pose and scene structure directly from radar measurements. We implement DiffRadar on commodity FMCW radar hardware and evaluate it on both the public Radarize benchmark and a controlled stress-test suite that targets common radar SLAM failure modes, including corridor degeneracy, motion regime transitions, dynamic clutter, and long-horizon loop closures. DiffRadar achieves substantial reductions in trajectory error on the benchmark, with especially large gains under feature-poor corridor motion, while more than doubling map consistency and maintaining real-time performance at 70 FPS. These results show that modeling radar observations directly in the signal domain enables substantially more robust and consistent radar-only SLAM for mobile platforms.

</details>


### [286] [Flatness-Preserving Residual Learning for Real-Time Tight Quadrotor Formation Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12275)
*Pei-An Hsieh,Fengjun Yang,Nikolai Matni,M. Ani Hsieh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息残差动力学学习框架，用于补偿多四旋翼紧密编队飞行中的气动干扰，在保持微分平坦性的同时实现高效反馈线性化控制，将跟踪误差降低31%，计算量比NMPC少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼在紧密编队飞行中受到湍流气动相互作用（如下沉气流）的严重影响，若不加建模可能导致灾难性碰撞，现有方法计算成本高或难以有效补偿这些效应。

Method: 提出物理信息残差动力学学习框架：学习复杂气动相互作用同时确保多四旋翼联合系统保持微分平坦性，利用保留的平坦性设计反馈线性化控制器，通过前馈补偿消除气动扰动。

Result: 相比基线方法平均跟踪误差降低31%；匹配NMPC的跟踪性能但计算量少一个数量级；仅需不到30秒训练数据和5ms循环速率即可实现稳定紧密编队飞行。

Conclusion: 首次证明在计算受限的飞行堆栈上，利用极少量训练数据和低计算开销即可实现高保真气动补偿的稳定紧密编队飞行。

Abstract: Quadrotors flying in tight formations are severely affected by turbulent aerodynamic interactions, such as downwash, that can cause catastrophic collisions if left unmodeled. To compensate for these effects, we propose a physics-informed residual dynamics learning framework that captures complex aerodynamic interactions while ensuring the joint multi-quadrotor system remains differentially flat. We leverage this preserved flatness to design a computationally efficient feedback linearization controller that is easily tunable with linear control techniques and cancels aerodynamic disturbances via feedforward compensation. Hardware experiments demonstrate our framework reduces average tracking errors by 31% compared to nominal baselines. Crucially, our lightweight approach matches the tracking performance of state-of-the-art nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) while requiring an order of magnitude less computation. We are the first to show that stable, tight formation flight can be achieved with under 30 seconds of training data and a 5ms loop rate, unlocking high-fidelity aerodynamic compensation for compute-constrained flight stacks.

</details>


### [287] [Reducing Temporal Redundancy for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12287)
*Yuzhou Wu,Yuxin Zheng,Muchun Niu,Yishan Yang,Tianhao Liu,hanwen kang,Jiajian Jing,Linfeng Zhang,Chuan Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种系统级加速策略，通过增量视觉编码（仅更新动态区域）和压缩扩散采样（2步采样）实现VLA模型推理速度2倍以上提升，在多个基准上保持高达98%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中泛化能力强，但高推理延迟限制了实时部署；现有VLA管线存在两类时间冗余：连续相似帧的重复视觉编码和扩散策略的多步迭代采样。

Method: 感知侧：只增量更新动态场景区域的token，而非重新编码整帧；动作侧：通过效率导向训练将扩散采样压缩为紧凑的2步调度，同时保持动作精度。

Result: 在Libero、RobotWin和真实机器人平台上实现2倍以上加速，在通用操作基准上成功率高达98%。

Conclusion: 系统级加速策略能有效降低VLA模型推理延迟，适用于真实机器人实时部署，代码将开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit strong generalization for robotic manipulation, yet their high inference latency limits real time deployment. We identify two primary sources of temporal redundancy in existing VLA pipelines: repeated visual encoding of highly similar consecutive frames and multi step iterative sampling in diffusion based policies. To address this, we propose a system level acceleration strategy that reduces computation in both perception and action generation. On the perception side, we incrementally update only tokens corresponding to dynamic scene regions instead of re-encoding entire frames. On the policy side, we compress diffusion sampling into a compact 2-step schedule through efficiency oriented training while preserving action precision. Experiments on Libero, RobotWin, and Real Robot Platforms demonstrate over 2 times speedup while maintaining high performance, achieving up to 98% success rate on general manipulation benchmarks. Our codes will be released on Github.

</details>


### [288] [VistaVLA: Geometry- and Semantic-Aware 3D Gaussian-Grounded VLA for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12356)
*Mohan Liu,Zhihao Gu,Xuanyu Chen,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Yan Wu,Wei-Yun Yau,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VistaVLA提出了一种两阶段框架，通过3D高斯原语构建几何-语义感知的3D认知表征，并压缩为紧凑上下文令牌用于VLA策略学习，在真实世界任务中成功率提升22.8%，在分布外任务上比VLA-Adapter基线提升30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏显式的场景级3D表示，限制了空间布局和几何约束的推理能力；而引入3D线索的方法仅捕获低级结构，缺乏3D空间中的高级语义接地。受人类3D语义认知地图启发，作者希望构建一种融合几何与语义的3D认知表征。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 将多视图视觉-语言特征提升为3D高斯原语，形成几何锚定的语义令牌，对齐视图一致的3D空间接地与2D视觉特征空间；2) 提出Merge-then-Query (MtQ)令牌摘要机制，将密集高斯原语压缩为紧凑的空间信息令牌，实现99%令牌缩减同时保留动作相关的3D布局和语义上下文。

Result: 在真实场景中，VistaVLA在7个任务上成功率提升22.8%；在具有挑战性的分布外任务上比VLA-Adapter基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: 显式构建几何和语义感知的3D认知表征能够显著提升VLA策略的空间推理能力和操作性能，MtQ机制有效解决了3D表征的计算效率问题。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a powerful end-to-end paradigm for robotic manipulation by mapping language instructions and 2D visual inputs directly to actions. However, these models lack an explicit, scene-level 3D representation, limiting their ability to reason over spatial layouts and geometric constraints. While recent efforts incorporate explicit 3D cues, such as depth maps or point clouds, to improve geometric awareness, they primarily capture low-level structures and lack high-level semantic grounding in 3D space. In human cognition, interaction with the physical world relies on a 3D semantic cognitive map - an internal mental model that integrates spatial layouts with semantic context to enable persistent, viewpoint-invariant reasoning. In light of this, we present VistaVLA, a novel two-stage framework that constructs a geometry- and semantics-aware 3D cognitive representation from 3D Gaussian primitives and grounds it as compact context tokens for VLA policy learning. Specifically, VistaVLA lifts multi-view vision-language features into 3D Gaussian primitives, forming geometry-anchored semantic tokens that align view-consistent spatial grounding with 2D visual feature spaces. To make this 3D representation computationally tractable for effective VLA control, we introduce Merge-then-Query (MtQ), a token summarization mechanism. MtQ compresses dense Gaussian primitives into a highly compact set of spatially informative tokens, achieving a 99% token reduction while preserving action-relevant 3D layouts and semantic context. Extensive evaluations in both simulated and real-world environments demonstrate the effectiveness of VistaVLA. Notably, in real-world scenarios, VistaVLA improves success rates by 22.8% across seven real-world tasks and by 30.0% over the VLA-Adapter baseline on challenging out-of-distribution tasks.

</details>


### [289] [StratMamba: Strategic and Reactive Stream Partitioning for Path-Efficient LiDAR-Based Obstacle Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12370)
*Hung-Chieh Wu,Xiaopan Zhang,Kasra Sinaei,Ryan Abnavi,Kasun Weerakoon,Christopher Bradley,Seyed Fakoorian,Jiachen Li,Donald Ebeigbe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StratMamba提出了一种双流Mamba时序建模架构，结合快速衰减和慢速衰减记忆，用于高效处理机器人导航中的长时域依赖，在仿真和真实场景中均优于LSTM、Transformer和Vanilla-Mamba基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序模型（如LSTM、Transformer）在复杂障碍环境中难以高效捕获机器人导航所需的长时域依赖，而Vanilla-Mamba虽具优势但无法同时兼顾高频避障与长程策略规划。

Method: 提出StratMamba双流架构：快速衰减记忆处理高频LiDAR数据用于反应式避障，慢速衰减记忆维护长程目标信息用于策略规划，两者协同工作。

Result: 在IsaacLab和Gazebo仿真中，StratMamba导航速度最快（576中位步数，比Vanilla-Mamba提升5.0%），超时率最低，路径最优性最高（0.915路径效率）。真实场景中，其鲁棒性优于Vanilla-Mamba和Transformer。

Conclusion: 双流记忆划分能有效平衡反应式安全性与策略性导航，尤其在挑战性感知条件下表现出更强的鲁棒性，成功实现从仿真到真实环境的迁移部署。

Abstract: This paper proposes StratMamba, a dual-stream Mamba-based temporal modeling architecture, to more efficiently capture long-horizon temporal dependencies required for robot navigation in complex and obstacle-rich environments. StratMamba leverages a combination of fast-decay and slow-decay memory architectures, where the fast-decay component processes high-frequency LiDAR data for reactive obstacle avoidance, while the slow-decay component maintains longer-horizon goal information for strategic planning. We perform extensive evaluations of different obstacle avoidance scenarios in IsaacLab and Gazebo, while also validating successful sim-to-real deployment on a Unitree GO1 quadruped robot navigating in the presence of static/dynamic obstacles. Comparisons with other temporal RL baselines, such as LSTM, Transformer, and Vanilla-Mamba, show that our StratMamba achieves exceptional temporal reasoning efficiency with a lower timeout rate, while maintaining the fastest navigation speed (576 median steps, 5.0% better than Vanilla-Mamba). It also achieves the highest path optimality (0.915 path efficiency) across all baselines. Real-world evaluation reveals that StratMamba maintains more robust performance across extended LiDAR ranges compared to vanilla Mamba and the Transformer, demonstrating that dual-stream partitioning effectively balances reactive safety with strategic navigation under challenging sensing conditions.

</details>


### [290] [Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control for Multi-Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12423)
*Zhilin He,Yorai Shaoul,Jiaoyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MDOC，一种无需数据、结合模型预测与控制障碍函数的扩散规划器，能高效生成多机器人连续环境下的动态可行、无碰撞轨迹，在样本效率、平滑度、成功率和计算时间上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的多机器人运动规划方法依赖大量演示数据，且难以在采样过程中严格施加动力学约束和硬安全约束，亟需一种无需数据、能严格保证动态可行性与安全性的方法。

Method: 将已知动力学模型与控制障碍函数约束投影结合到扩散模型的采样过程中，并通过基于冲突的搜索（CBS）将安全机制自然扩展到多机器人规划场景。

Result: 在仿真实验中，MDOC在样本效率、轨迹几何平滑度、成功率上一致优于代表性基线方法，同时降低了计算时间并生成了无碰撞轨迹。

Conclusion: MDOC通过模型驱动的扩散最优控制，有效解决了多机器人运动规划中动态可行性与硬安全约束的难题，且无需训练数据，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Multi-Robot Motion Planning in continuous environments, where robots must generate dynamically feasible, collision-free trajectories, is challenging due to the combinatorial growth of the joint trajectory space and the difficulty of enforcing dynamic feasibility and hard safety constraints. Recent approaches recast trajectory planning as probabilistic inference, sampling from a posterior over trajectories using diffusion models whose score functions are learned from demonstration data. While showing promising performance, these approaches are limited: they often rely on sizable demonstration datasets and struggle to rigorously enforce dynamics and hard safety constraints during sampling. To this end, we introduce Model-Based Diffusion Optimal Control (MDOC), a model-based diffusion planner that efficiently produces dynamically feasible trajectories without relying on data. Crucially, we show that MDOC's safety mechanism -- combining known dynamics models with Control Barrier Function-constrained projections -- naturally scales to multi-robot planning settings through Conflict-Based Search. Across simulation experiments, this integrated method consistently outperforms representative baseline planners in sample efficiency, geometric smoothness, and success rate, while reducing computation time and producing collision-free trajectories.

</details>


### [291] [Deployable Human Preference Alignment in Robotics: Learning Representative Rewards from Diverse Human Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12466)
*Taehyung Kim,Gwangmo Lee,Minjun Chang,Sunghyun Lim,Jongeun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PREC是一种通过偏好奖励聚类学习紧凑策略集的框架，能在稀疏、噪声偏好反馈下实现用户对齐，兼顾群体偏好多样性与部署验证效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有用户对齐方法中，单用户对齐策略面临反馈稀疏、噪声敏感、策略验证负担重的问题；单一共享策略则无法捕捉异质偏好并忽视少数群体偏好。亟需一种既能处理稀疏噪声反馈、又能捕获多样化偏好、同时降低验证成本的方案。

Method: PREC框架首先将用户偏好标签搁置，跨用户聚合轨迹学习群体共享轨迹编码器（避免标签噪声干扰）；然后在该表示基础上联合执行用户聚类（偏好一致簇）和每个簇的奖励模型学习，再为每个簇优化策略。聚类补偿了单用户标签稀疏性并缓解噪声影响，有限数量的奖励模型降低了部署验证负担。

Result: 在多个模拟 locomotion 环境中，PREC比基线方法更准确地将标记不同轨迹子集的用户分组到偏好一致簇中。在稀疏和噪声反馈下，PREC训练的策略在所有三个社会福利指标上均优于现有的单共享策略用户对齐方法，甚至超过了单用户对齐方法。

Conclusion: PREC通过偏好聚类有效平衡了用户对齐中的个性化与泛化问题，证明了在稀疏噪声反馈下聚类策略比单用户策略和单共享策略更具优势，兼顾了偏好多样性、鲁棒性和部署可行性。

Abstract: Aligning robot policies with human preferences is essential for deployment to diverse end users. In per-user alignment approach, preference feedback is often sparse, so learning becomes unstable and vulnerable to human preference noise, and a growing number of individualized policies makes validation difficult before deployment. A single shared policy approach to user alignment avoids this cost but fails to capture heterogeneous preferences and often neglects minority preferences. To address these challenges, we introduce Preference-based REward Clustering (PREC), a novel framework that learns a compact set of policies from binary preference labels provided by diverse users. From a dataset of user trajectories and their preference labels, PREC first sets the labels aside and aggregates trajectories across users to learn a population-level shared trajectory encoder, alleviating limited per-user coverage and avoiding label noise during representation learning. Using this representation, PREC jointly assigns users to preference-coherent clusters and learns a representative reward model per cluster using preference labels, from which a policy is optimized for each cluster. Clustering similar users compensates for the limited number of labels available from each user and mitigates the effect of label noise. At the same time, maintaining a manageable number of reward models reduces the validation burden at deployment. Experiments across diverse simulated locomotion environments show that PREC groups users who label different trajectory subsets into preference-coherent clusters more accurately than baseline methods. Under sparse and noisy feedback, policies trained with PREC improve all three social welfare metrics over an existing single shared-policy user-alignment approach and even outperform per-user alignment approaches.

</details>


### [292] [Infra-Swarm: Robust Vision-Based Multi-Robot Swarming via Near-Infrared Spectral Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12489)
*Haoyu Chen,Qijin Li,Wanyu Xiang,Xiuxiu Lin,Zian Ning,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Infra-Swarm提出了一种基于近红外视觉的鲁棒分布式集群系统，利用940nm窄带滤波器和光学光斑的3D位置估计，实现了厘米级定位和硬件级抗干扰能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式集群依赖主动无线通信（受带宽限制）或被动视觉（受环境敏感度影响），缺乏兼顾鲁棒性和可扩展性的解决方案。

Method: 每个机器人配备近红外光源和四个灰度相机，通过图像中光斑的位置（朝向）和强度（距离）直接计算邻居的厘米级3D位置，并利用940nm窄带滤波器物理滤除99.2%的环境光干扰。

Result: 实现了硬件级别的抗光照变化鲁棒性，极低计算开销，为资源受限硬件上的大规模机器人集群提供了基础。

Conclusion: Infra-Swarm是一种鲁棒的视觉集群方案，在抗环境干扰和可扩展性方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Distributed swarms typically rely on either active wireless communication or passive vision, and they are frequently hindered by bandwidth constraints or environmental sensitivity. This paper proposes Infra-Swarm, a robust vision-based swarm. Each robot is equipped with a near-infrared light source and four ordinary gray-scale cameras. The Infra-Swarm system directly measures the centimeter-level 3D position of neighbors based on the position (bearing) and intensity (strength) of optical flares in the captured images. By utilizing 940 nm narrow-band filters to physically reject 99.2% of ambient light interference, the perception front-end achieves hardware-level robustness against illumination variations. Furthermore, its minimal computational overhead provides a resilient foundation for the massive scalability of robotic collectives on resource-constrained hardware.

</details>


### [293] [A Bearing-Strength Method for Motion Estimation of Unknown Energy Emitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12515)
*Haoyu Chen,Zian Ning,Yin Zhang,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合方位测量与信号强度的被动运动估计方法，解决了传统仅利用方位信息方法对传感器横向运动的依赖问题，显著增强了系统可观测性，且无需额外成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统被动传感器的仅方位运动估计方法要求传感器必须具有横向运动才能保证可观测性，这种限制与许多任务中传感器的期望运动模式相冲突。信号强度信息虽易获取却未被充分利用，本文旨在利用这一信息弥补现有方法的不足。

Method: 提出一种方位-强度联合方法，同时利用被动传感器获取的方位向量和接收信号强度信息进行运动估计，并通过理论分析证明该方法无需传感器横向运动即可保证系统可观测性。

Result: 理论分析表明新方法显著增强了系统可观测性，消除了对横向运动条件的要求；真实世界实验验证了所提方法和理论分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 利用以往被忽略的信号强度信息可有效增强被动运动估计性能，且该方法不增加额外硬件成本，具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper studies motion estimation of moving energy emitters using passive sensors. The emitters may be light, acoustic, or radio sources. While the bearing vector pointing from the sensor to the emitter can be easily obtained, existing approaches mainly rely on the bearing-only motion estimation method. However, this method suffers from a fundamental limitation that the sensor must have lateral motion to ensure observability. Unfortunately, this lateral motion requirement often conflicts with the sensor's desired motion in many tasks. In this paper, we point out that the received signal strength, which can also be obtained easily in many ways, can greatly enhance motion estimation. Surprisingly, this strength information has not been well explored so far. Here, we propose a new bearing-strength method to fully exploit both the bearing and strength measurements. Our theoretical analysis shows that the system observability is significantly enhanced in the sense that the lateral motion condition is not required anymore. Real-world experimental results verify the proposed method and the theoretical analysis. It is notable that the benefit of the proposed method comes with no additional cost since it simply utilizes the received strength information that has not been fully exploited in the past.

</details>


### [294] [Mind the Gap: Promises and Pitfalls of Hierarchical Planning in LeWorldModel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12547)
*Niccolò Caselli,Salvatore Lo Sardo,Francesco Massafra,Ippokratis Pantelidis,Samuele Punzo,Sathya Kamesh Bhethanabhotla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在长期目标条件控制任务中，时间层次结构是否能改善LeWorldModel（LeWM）。作者提出Hi-LeWM扩展，冻结预训练的低层LeWM并添加高层潜在子目标规划。实验表明，层次结构并非自动提升性能：短视距下单步高层最优，长视距下高层动作空间与搜索分布不匹配。通过约束搜索到训练轨迹编码的宏动作并调整子目标执行时序，可在中等和最长视距下分别提升+11.3和+14.7个百分点。结论：时间抽象对紧凑冻结LeWM有益，但前提是高层搜索与低层控制器兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究时间层次结构（temporal hierarchy）是否能提升LeWorldModel在长期目标条件控制任务中的性能，并识别高层规划与低层控制器之间的匹配瓶颈。

Method: 提出Hi-LeWM，冻结预训练的低层LeWM，添加高层规划器进行潜在子目标规划。在PushT和Cube任务中，对不同目标偏移量进行实验，对比单步高层、无约束搜索、真值子目标以及约束搜索（使用训练轨迹编码的宏动作）等多种配置。

Result: 短视距下单步高层最优；长视距下高层动作空间与搜索分布不匹配。真值子目标实验表明冻结低层控制器执行良好，瓶颈在于高层子目标生成。约束搜索配合适当子目标执行时序，在中等视距提升+11.3pp，最长PushT视距提升+14.7pp。

Conclusion: 时间抽象能改善紧凑冻结的LeWM，但仅当高层搜索与低层控制器兼容时才有效。高层子目标生成是主要瓶颈，需约束搜索空间以匹配低层能力。

Abstract: We investigate whether temporal hierarchy can improve LeWorldModel on long-horizon goal-conditioned control. We introduce Hi-LeWM, an extension that freezes the pretrained low-level LeWM and adds high-level planning over latent subgoals. We evaluate Hi-LeWM on PushT and Cube across increasing goal offsets. Hierarchy does not automatically improve performance: at short horizons, the best configuration uses a one-step high-level horizon, while longer horizons reveal a mismatch between the learned high-level action space and the inference-time search distribution. Experiments with true future latent subgoals show that the frozen low-level controller can execute well-aligned intermediate targets, indicating that high-level subgoal generation is the main bottleneck. Unconstrained search can select latent macro-actions that appear favorable under the learned model but produce poor control targets. Constraining search around macro-actions encoded from training trajectories, with appropriate subgoal execution timing, recovers useful hierarchical regimes, improving over flat LeWM by +11.3 percentage points at medium-range horizons and +14.7 percentage points at the longest PushT horizon. Overall, temporal abstraction can benefit compact frozen LeWM, but only when high-level search remains compatible with the low-level controller

</details>


### [295] [TrustVLA: Mechanism-Guided Inference-Time Defense Against Vision-Language-Action Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12571)
*Pinhan Fu,Xianda Guo,Xuetao Li,Wenke Huang,Ruilin Wang,Weiheng Zhao,Wei Sui,Mang Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在后门攻击下存在一种共同的内部机制——紧凑因果足迹（compact causal footprint），并提出了一种无需重训练的推理时防御方法TrustVLA，通过基于Dirichlet证据框架的不确定性监测、因果定位和局部修复来检测和防御视觉触发的后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉或语言防御方法很少能解释被触发的VLA表示的特征，也无法在不重训练的情况下恢复模型行为。用户无法审计VLA模型部署流水线，而被植入后门的VLA模型在干净观测下表现正常，但微小视觉触发器会重定向长期机器人策略，且故障在发生前难以被观测到。

Method: 首先识别出被攻击VLA模型中的共同内部机制——紧凑因果足迹：视觉支持区域小、被注意力机制选择、空间紧凑，且在因果意义上掩码该区域可使证据演化分数回归正常。基于此提出TrustVLA：将可信分类中的Dirichlet证据框架适配到VLA策略中，逐token、逐层监测认知不确定性，通过反事实机制分数下降定位紧凑支持区域，并通过局部修复恢复观测。

Result: 在OpenVLA/LIBERO和π0.5迁移评估中，TrustVLA降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了干净任务性能。

Conclusion: TrustVLA提供了一种无需重训练、机制引导的防御方法，可用于视觉触发的VLA后门攻击检测与防御。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are deployed through pipelines that end users cannot audit, and a poisoned VLA can behave normally on clean observations while a small visual trigger redirects a long-horizon robot policy before any failure becomes observable. Existing vision or language defenses rarely explain what a triggered VLA representation looks like or how to recover behavior without retraining. We study this gap through two independently proposed VLA attacks from groups with distinct injection strategies, BadVLA and INFUSE; the latter persists after downstream clean adaptation. Across the evaluated poisoned models, we identify a recurring internal mechanism: a \emph{compact causal footprint}, namely a small visual support that is attention-seeded, spatially compact, and \emph{causal} in a precise sense -- masking it returns a clean-calibrated evidence-evolution score to the normal operating region. This footprint motivates TrustVLA, a mechanism-guided inference-time defense that adapts the Dirichlet evidence framework from trusted classification to monitor per-token, per-layer epistemic uncertainty in VLA policies. With only a small clean calibration set, TrustVLA (i)~detects abnormal evidence evolution, (ii)~localizes the compact support by counterfactual mechanism-score drop, and (iii)~recovers the observation by localized inpainting. Across OpenVLA/LIBERO and $π_{0.5}$ transfer evaluations, TrustVLA reduces attack success while preserving clean-task performance, providing a retraining-free, mechanism-guided defense for visual-triggered VLA backdoors.

</details>


### [296] [Streamlining stereo differentiable rendering for marker-free real-time tracking of surgical robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12604)
*Yanghe Hao,Martin Huber,Christos Bergeles,Tom Vercauteren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于立体可微渲染的无标记手术机器人实时位姿跟踪方法，通过顺序优化和CUDA并行加速，在1080p分辨率下实现30fps实时跟踪，精度达到1.7cm/0.6deg（静态）和1.2cm（动态），在遮挡场景下优于FoundationPose 63%，推理速度提升6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术环境中基于标记的机器人跟踪易受遮挡影响，存在安全隐患和设置繁琐问题，亟需无标记、抗遮挡的实时位姿跟踪方法以提升手术安全性和多机器人交互能力。

Method: 扩展无标记位姿估计框架roboreg至在线动态跟踪：(i) 顺序优化——跨帧传播位姿估计并采用运动自适应超参数调优；(ii) CUDA流并行化分割与优化，结合CUDA-graph加速分割。

Result: 在38个无遮挡和5个遮挡位移序列上，实现1080p/30fps实时跟踪（原14fps），静态精度1.7cm/0.6deg，动态平均3D误差1.2cm（27460帧），遮挡场景1.53cm（1242帧）。相比FoundationPose，动态估计提升11%（遮挡下63%），静态提升250%，推理速度6倍。

Conclusion: 立体可微渲染实现了实时、高分辨率、无标记的手术机器人跟踪，性能媲美基于标记的方法，并显著超越基础模型基线。

Abstract: Purpose: Marker-based tracking of surgical robots is occlusion-prone in cluttered operating rooms. We evaluate stereo differentiable rendering for marker-free, real-time robot pose tracking, potentially improving safety, reducing setup time, and enabling multi-robot interaction. Methods: We extend the markerless pose estimation framework roboreg to online dynamic tracking via (i) sequential optimisation that propagates pose estimates across frames with motion-adaptive hyperparameter tuning, and (ii) CUDA stream parallelisation of segmentation and optimisation, combined with CUDA-graph accelerated segmentation. We evaluate on 38 unobstructed and 5 occluded displacement sequences with static start/end ground-truth calibrations and dynamic marker-based reference tracking. Results: We achieve real-time 1080p tracking at 30 fps (up from 14 fps for vanilla roboreg), matching the camera frame rate. Accuracy reaches 1.7 cm / 0.6 deg against static ground truth and 1.2 cm mean 3D error over 27,460 frames against the marker-based reference (1.53 cm over 1,242 occluded frames). Our method outperforms FoundationPose by 11% in dynamic estimation (63% under occlusion) and 250% in static estimation, with 6x faster inference. Conclusions: Stereo differentiable rendering enables real-time, high-resolution marker-free surgical robot tracking, on par with marker-based approaches and surpassing foundation-model baselines.

</details>


### [297] [Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps for Visual Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12630)
*Jiho Hong,Eunae Kang,Sanghyun Kim,Young-Sik Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出实例增强语义地图（Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps），通过2.5D实例级语义建图、LLM驱动的鲁棒查询处理和存储高效表示，在VLN任务中显著提升了物体检索和导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN方法缺乏实例级对象细节，且对多样化用户查询的鲁棒性不足，限制了在复杂室内环境中的可靠导航。

Method: (1) 基于开放词汇全景分割构建2.5D实例级语义地图，保留垂直区分和微小物体，存储丰富语义属性和房间级上下文描述；(2) LLM驱动的目标选择，通过专家路由和分数级融合实现多样化查询的鲁棒处理；(3) 存储高效的语义表示，相比3D场景图减少约96%存储。

Result: 2.5D表示在预测归一化AUC上比3D基线提升超27%；导航实验中，物体检索提升超17%，导航成功率提升超23%。

Conclusion: 所提出的实例增强语义地图框架通过2.5D实例级建图和LLM查询处理，有效提升了VLN在复杂室内环境中的性能，同时大幅降低了存储开销。

Abstract: Visual Language Navigation (VLN) aims to enable an embodied agent to navigate complex environments by following natural language instructions. Recent approaches build semantic spatial maps and leverage Large Language Models (LLMs) for reasoning and decision making. Despite these advances, existing systems lack instance-level object detail and robustness to diverse user queries, limiting reliable navigation in complex indoor environments. To address these limitations, we propose Instance-Enriched Semantic Maps, a unified framework with three key contributions: (1) Instance-level two-and-a-half-dimensional (2.5D) rich information mapping that constructs maps from color and depth observations via open-vocabulary panoptic segmentation, preserving vertical distinctions and capturing small objects, while storing diverse semantic attributes and natural language captions enriched with room-level context. (2) Robust query processing via LLM-based target selection, which dynamically routes queries across type-specialized experts and integrates their outputs through score-level fusion, enabling consistent goal selection across diverse query formulations. (3) Storage-efficient semantic representation that achieves approximately 96% reduction compared to three-dimensional (3D) scene-graph approaches while preserving sufficient spatial information for navigation. The proposed 2.5D representation outperforms the 3D baseline by over 27% in prediction-normalized Area Under the Curve (AUC). In navigation experiments, our method achieves over 17% improvement in object retrieval and over 23% in navigation success compared to the baseline across diverse query types. The project page is available at https://rcilab.github.io/iesm_vln.

</details>


### [298] [Jetson-PI: Towards Onboard Real-Time Robot Control via Foresight-Aligned Asynchronous Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12659)
*Zebin Yang,Qi Wang,Yunhe Wang,Xiurui Guo,Bo Yu,Shaoshan Liu,Jiafeng Xu,Hao Dong,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Jetson-PI方法，通过前瞻对齐异步校正技术，解决VLA模型在低功耗边缘设备上部署时的感知-执行错位和长反应时间问题，在Jetson Orin上实现8.66倍控制频率提升，并在LIBERO基准上超越VLASH 14.8%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在边缘设备（如Jetson Orin）上部署时面临高计算复杂度导致的推理延迟大、控制频率低的问题。异步推理虽可部分掩盖延迟，但引入感知-执行错位和长反应时间两个关键问题。

Method: 提出Jetson-PI方法，包含三个核心组件：(1) 轻量级未来校正模块，预测以已执行动作为条件的未来环境表征，使动作专家直接预测未来时间步的动作；(2) 基于置信度的调度优化，自适应平衡VLM和动作专家的调用；(3) 系统级加速，包括CUDA图复用、GPU常驻中间缓冲和流展开。

Result: 在NVIDIA Jetson Orin上，Jetson-PI相比naive PyTorch和vla.cpp分别实现8.66倍和5.41倍的控制频率提升；在LIBERO基准上，平均成功率比VLASH高出14.8%。

Conclusion: Jetson-PI有效解决了VLA模型在低功耗边缘设备上部署的效率问题，通过前瞻对齐的异步校正机制兼顾了控制频率和任务成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved impressive performance on diverse embodied tasks. However, deploying VLA models on low-power onboard devices, such as the Jetson Orin, remains challenging due to their high computational complexity, which leads to substantial inference latency and low control frequency. Asynchronous inference can partially mask this latency by parallelizing action execution and subsequent inference, but it introduces two critical issues: perception-execution misalignment and long reaction time. In this paper, we propose Jetson-PI, a method for efficient VLA deployment on onboard devices via Foresight-Aligned Asynchronous Correction. To address misalignment, we train a lightweight future correction module that predicts future environment representation conditioned on committed actions, enabling the action expert to directly predict actions from the future time step. To reduce reaction time, we introduce confidence-based scheduling optimization that adaptively balances VLM and action expert invocations, complemented by system-level accelerations including CUDA graph reuse, GPU-resident intermediate buffering, and flow unrolling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Jetson-PI achieves 8.66x and 5.41x improvements in control frequency compared with naive PyTorch and vla.cpp on NVIDIA Jetson Orin, while outperforming VLASH by 14.8\% in average success rate on the LIBERO benchmark. The code of our asynchronous algorithm is available on https://github.com/PKU-SEC-Lab/Jetson-PI, and our efficient llama.cpp-based inference engine is available on https://github.com/PKU-SEC-Lab/Jetson-PI-Edge.

</details>


### [299] [Vision-Based Dribbling for Humanoid Soccer via Privileged Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12702)
*Flavio Maiorana,Valerio Spagnoli,Eugenio Bugli,Flavio Volpi,Daniele Affinita,Vincenzo Suriani,Daniele Nardi,Luca Iocchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将时间深度编码器嵌入强化学习策略的集成方法，使仿人机器人在仅依靠深度观测的情况下，无需显式状态估计即可学会基于视觉、感知对手的带球技能。在仿真Booster T1仿人机器人上，该方法在目标驱动带球中达到100%成功率，单静态障碍下96%，面对主动移动抢球对手时达到46%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仿人机器人带球方法通常将感知与运动分离，在遮挡、快速球运动和复杂对手交互场景下，由于感知未直接针对控制进行优化而容易失败。

Method: 提出一种集成方法，通过任务特定投影层将时间深度编码器嵌入强化学习策略中，使机器人直接从深度观测中学习基于视觉、感知对手的带球技能，无需显式状态估计或特权场景信息。

Result: 在仿真Booster T1仿人机器人上，目标驱动带球成功率达100%，单静态障碍下96%，面对主动移动抢球对手时达46%。

Conclusion: 该框架支持在标称和中动态场景下实现鲁棒的基于视觉的带球能力，并为处理更具挑战性的移动对手场景提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in humanoid robotics have highlighted the importance of deployable loco-manipulation skills. Dribbling a soccer ball while evading active opponents requires simultaneous balance, precise ball control, and awareness of a dynamic adversary under onboard sensing and real-time constraints. Existing approaches typically separate perception and motion, which can be effective in controlled settings but may fail under occlusions, fast ball movements, and complex opponent interactions, since perception is not directly optimized for control. We propose an integrated approach in which a temporal depth encoder is embedded into a reinforcement learning policy through a task-specific projection layer. We apply this framework to a simulated Booster T1 humanoid robot and show that it is possible to learn vision-based, opponent-aware dribbling directly from depth observations, without explicit state estimation or privileged scene information. The learned policy achieves 100% success in nominal target-driven dribbling and 96% success with a single static obstacle, while reaching 46% success against an actively moving ball-attacker opponent. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework supports robust vision-based dribbling in nominal and moderately dynamic settings, and provides a strong foundation for handling more challenging moving-adversary scenarios.

</details>


### [300] [Globalized Constrained Stein Variational Inference for Diverse Feasible Robot Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12732)
*Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SteinSQP方法，将Stein变分推理与序列二次规划结合，用于约束环境下机器人运动的多样化可行采样，在保持多样性的同时确保所有样本满足运动学约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人运动规划本质上是多模态的，但经典规划器通常只返回单一解。概率方法虽可维持运动分布，但机器人运动样本必须满足严格约束（碰撞避免、关节限位、接触条件、动力学一致性），使得多样化可行运动采样极具挑战性。

Method: 提出SteinSQP，将约束直接嵌入核空间SQP子问题，采用GPU友好的矩阵自由原始-对偶算法求解约束Stein-Newton子问题，并引入集成级别评价函数平衡目标值、约束违反和粒子多样性。

Result: 在五个约束运动规划任务中，SteinSQP返回完全可行的集成解并保持多样化运动方案。相比一阶约束Stein基线和串行多起点非线性规划，在迭代次数、粒子可行性、批处理时间上均表现更优。

Conclusion: SteinSQP能有效解决约束环境下多样化可行运动采样问题，兼顾可行性与多样性，且具有高效的计算性能。

Abstract: Robot motion planning is inherently multimodal, yet classical planners typically return only a single solution. Probabilistic formulations address this limitation by maintaining a distribution over motions, allowing the planner to reason over multiple low-cost alternatives. In robotics, however, motion samples must also satisfy strict constraints, including collision avoidance, joint limits, contact conditions, and dynamics consistency. These hard requirements make motion sampling substantially more challenging: within a limited planning budget, the ensemble must cover diverse low-cost motions while ensuring that every sample remains feasible under the relevant constraints. We propose SteinSQP (Stein Variational Sequential Quadratic Programming), a constrained Stein variational inference method for diverse feasible robot motion sampling. SteinSQP evolves an interacting particle ensemble, as in Stein variational methods, while embedding constraints directly into a kernel-space SQP subproblem. We solve the resulting constrained Stein-Newton subproblem with a GPU-friendly matrix-free primal-dual algorithm, enabling efficient batched ensemble updates. To globalize the method, we introduce an ensemble-level merit function that jointly balances objective value, constraint violation, and particle diversity. Across five constrained motion-planning tasks, SteinSQP returns fully feasible ensembles while preserving diverse motion alternatives. Compared with first-order constrained Stein baselines and serial multistart nonlinear programming, SteinSQP shows faster and more robust ensemble convergence in terms of iterations, improves particle-wise feasibility, and achieves faster batched time-to-solution on challenging robot-scale tasks.

</details>


### [301] [Directional Constraints for Efficient Exploration in Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12784)
*Paolo Magliano,Puze Liu,Jan Peters,Davide Tateo,Raffaello Camoriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ATACOM-DC方法，通过引入方向性约束区分接近/远离约束边界的动作，仅在必要时激活约束强制执行，从而改善安全强化学习中的安全-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在机器人领域表现优异，但实际部署需要强安全保证。现有安全强化学习方法在约束下学习会降低学习速度并导致次优任务性能，因为智能体需要求解更复杂的约束优化问题。

Method: 基于ATACOM安全层框架进行扩展，提出方向性约束（Directional Constraints），区分接近约束边界的动作和远离约束边界的动作，仅在必要时才激活约束强制执行。

Result: 在多种具有挑战性的机器人控制任务仿真中评估了约束违规成本和任务性能。

Conclusion: ATACOM-DC显著改善了安全-性能权衡，通过引入方向性约束避免了不必要的约束激活，从而在保证安全的同时提升任务性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning has revolutionized the landscape of robotic research, allowing robust learning of complex robotic skills in simulation. However, real-world deployment in open-ended environments requires strong safety guarantees to prevent dangerous or harmful behaviors. Safe Reinforcement Learning methods address this requirement by enforcing safety constraints. Nevertheless, learning under constraints often reduces learning speed and could lead to suboptimal task performance, as the agent must solve a more complex constrained optimization problem compared to unconstrained settings. To tackle this issue, in this work, we propose an extension of the ATACOM framework, a state-of-the-art reliable safety layer that can be integrated with existing Reinforcement Learning algorithms to enforce constraints derived from prior knowledge of the system or learned directly from data. Our proposed method, named ATACOM Directional Constraints (ATACOM-DC), significantly improves the safety-performance trade-off by introducing directional constraints that distinguish between actions approaching and moving away from constraint boundaries, activating constraint enforcement only when necessary. We evaluate our method across a range of challenging robotic control tasks in simulation, analyzing both constraint-violation costs and achieved task performance. Code and additional material at https://atacom-dc.robot-learning.net.

</details>


### [302] [Autonomous Tracking and Terminal Guidance of Moving Targets for Fixed-Wing UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12801)
*Wei-Hao Liou,Teng-Hu Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向固定翼无人机与云台相机的统一控制框架，涵盖从目标检测到终端拦截的完整任务流程，结合YOLO检测、UKF状态估计、带CBF约束的NMPC跟踪以及四元数BPNG末端制导，仿真验证了框架的鲁棒性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有固定翼无人机跟踪框架缺乏从目标检测到精确终端拦截的端到端统一控制方案，且在跟踪过程中难以处理自遮挡（self-occlusion）这一常见问题，导致视觉丢失和目标状态估计不稳定。

Method: 框架分为三阶段：1）基于视觉的目标获取阶段（YOLO检测）；2）基于UKF融合视觉与惯性测量进行目标状态估计，并采用引入Control Barrier Functions（CBFs）的约束感知NMPC策略防止自遮挡；3）满足终端条件后切换至四元数Biased Proportional Navigation Guidance（BPNG）实现精确撞击角约束的终端制导。

Result: 高保真仿真表明，该框架在严格遵循飞行器动力学限制和相机视场角约束的前提下，实现了稳定鲁棒的跟踪与精确的终端拦截。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一控制框架能够有效解决固定翼无人机在目标跟踪中的自遮挡问题，并在完整任务链条上实现从检测到拦截的鲁棒控制，具有实用价值。

Abstract: This study introduces a unified control framework for fixed-wing unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) fitted with a pan-tilt (PT) camera, intended to perform an end-to-end mission spanning from initial target detection to accurate terminal engagement. The proposed system employs a three-phase strategy: a vision-based target acquisition phase, an NMPC-based tracking phase, and a terminal guidance phase. During tracking, the framework uses an Unscented Kalman Filter (UKF) to fuse YOLO-based visual detections with inertial measurements, enabling robust target state estimation under unknown dynamics. To ensure reliable visual contact, we introduce a constraint-aware Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) strategy that incorporates Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) to explicitly prevent UAV self-occlusion -- a common limitation in fixed-wing tracking. Upon satisfying terminal engagement conditions, the system seamlessly transitions control to a quaternion-based Biased Proportional Navigation Guidance (BPNG) law, enforcing precise impact angle constraints. High-fidelity simulations demonstrate that the framework achieves stable, robust tracking and accurate terminal interception while strictly respecting the vehicle's dynamic limits and camera field-of-view constraints.

</details>


### [303] [PixelLoop: Shortcut Topological Navigation with Pixel-Level Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12811)
*Sarthak Chittawar,Vansh Garg,Aditya Vadali,Krish Pandya,Rohit Jayanti,Sourav Garg,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PixelLoop提出了一种在像素空间直接进行闭环检测的方法，通过在密集像素拓扑图中建立密集拓扑捷径（而非传统SLAM中的位姿图校正或图像级边），显著改善了拓扑导航的规划连通性和代价传播，在仿真和真实机器人实验中均取得了超过35%的成功率和SPL绝对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑地图中闭环检测的具体作用及其在下游导航任务中的影响尚未被充分研究；传统闭环方法在纯拓扑表示（区别于度量地图和全局轨迹闭环）中的角色与效果尚不明确。

Method: 基于像素级相对3D几何的密集拓扑结构，在像素空间直接引入闭环连接（PixelLoop），形成密集拓扑捷径，改变规划连通性与代价传播，而非仅对齐坐标。

Result: 在仿真实验中，相较于图像级基线，成功率（Success Rate）和SPL均获得超过35%的绝对提升；在真实机器人部署中验证了方法的实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 像素级密集闭环连接为拓扑视觉导航提供了一种实用且鲁棒的基础，其优势在需要利用捷径的场景中尤为显著。

Abstract: Although topological mapping and navigation have been studied extensively, the specific role and downstream effect of loop closures in purely topological representations has received relatively little attention. Importantly, loop closure over topological maps is distinct from loop closure over globally referenced trajectories and metric maps. Building on recent denser topologies grounded in pixel-level, relative 3D geometry, we propose PixelLoop which introduces loop closures directly in pixel space. Unlike sparse image-level edges or pose-graph corrections in SLAM, our pixel-level closures act as dense topological shortcuts that alter planning connectivity and cost propagation rather than merely aligning coordinates. This dense connectivity enables stable any-point-to-any-point navigation and produces costmaps that align accurately with geometric shortest paths. In particular, we showcase the distinct advantage of applying loop closures to fine-grained pixel topologies rather than image-level topologies. Across extensive simulated experiments, PixelLoop achieves over 35% absolute improvement in both Success Rate and SPL compared to image-relative baselines, with the largest gains in scenarios requiring shortcut exploitation. Results are further validated through real-world mobile robot deployments, demonstrating that dense pixel-level loop closures provide a practical and robust foundation for topological visual navigation. Project Page: https://pixelloop-nav.github.io/

</details>


### [304] [Unveiling Complex Collective Behaviors from Simple Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12861)
*Yize Mi,Jianan Li,Liang Li,Shiyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出EEC解释框架，包含Agent Response Map (ARM)分析工具，用于揭示机器人集群多智能体强化学习（MARL）策略中隐式的几何场学习机制。ARM发现机器人在合作形状组装任务中将未占用目标内部作为导航目标，在竞争性捕食-逃避任务中，猎物将捕食者Voronoi图边界作为汇聚目标，从而揭示了简单奖励与涌现集体行为之间的隐藏机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MARL神经策略的黑箱特性限制了机器人集群的战略分析与多机器人应用，且复杂集群行为可能从简单奖励中涌现而无需显式聚合激励，但简单奖励与集体行为之间的脱节加剧了可解释性挑战，亟需揭示这一涌现过程中的隐藏机制。

Method: 提出两阶段EEC解释框架，核心创新为Agent Response Map (ARM)分析工具，该工具能够揭示智能体在空间上的决策模式，识别聚合与回避区域，从而发现机器人隐式学习环境几何场并将其用作协调运动的目标。

Result: 在合作形状组装任务中，ARM识别出未占用的目标内部区域为机器人的期望导航目的地；在竞争性捕食-逃避任务中，ARM发现猎物将捕食者Voronoi图的边界作为汇聚目的地。两个任务均验证了ARM发现MARL策略中隐藏几何结构的能力。

Conclusion: ARM能够有效揭示机器人集群中MARL策略所隐式学习的几何结构，为理解简单奖励驱动下涌现的复杂集群行为提供了可解释性分析工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) holds great potential for robot swarms, but the black-box nature of neural policies complicates strategic analysis, limiting multi-robot applications. Furthermore, complex swarm behaviors can surprisingly emerge from simple rewards without explicit aggregation incentives. Unveiling the mechanisms behind this emergence is critical, but the disconnection between simple rewards and collective behaviors exacerbates interpretability challenges. This paper aims to reveal the hidden mechanisms in this process. We propose a two-stage EEC (\LinkIII) explanatory framework. This includes a novel analytical tool called the Agent Response Map (ARM), which reveals agents' decision-making patterns across space and identifies regions of aggregation and avoidance. ARM reveals that the robots implicitly learn the geometric fields of the environment and utilize these structures as desired targets for coordinated movement. We validate this finding across two distinct tasks: a cooperative multi-robot shape assembly and a competitive predator-prey pursuit-evasion. 1) In the cooperative task, ARM identifies the unoccupied target interior as the desired destination for robot navigation. As the center becomes occupied, this target region automatically shifts toward the boundary, demonstrating the robots' capacity to autonomously explore unoccupied areas. 2) In the competitive task, ARM surprisingly identifies the boundary of the predators' Voronoi diagram as the convergence destination for prey agents. Together, these two tasks demonstrate the capability of ARM to discover the hidden geometric structures underlying MARL policies in robot swarms.

</details>


### [305] [UR-VC: Unsupervised Robotic Value Correction for Time-Derived Progress Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12892)
*Lirui Zhao,Modi Shi,Li Chen,Qi Liu,Ping Luo,Hongyang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出UR-VC，一种无需训练、离线的方法，利用跨轨迹相似状态聚合来修正时间衍生的进度标签，在接触丰富操作任务中比原始时间标签更好地反映实际物理进度，并用于VLA优势条件策略学习，初步实验显示正向趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器人学习依赖密集进度/价值信号，但通常使用标准化时间作为替代标签，这在接触丰富操作任务中是有噪声的——时间单调增长而实际物理进度可能反复丢失（滑动、抓取失败等），因此需要一种无需人工标注的方法来修正此类信号。

Method: 离线、无训练方法，利用跨轨迹相似状态检索与聚合：从其他episode中检索与当前状态相似的状态，聚合它们的时间标签作为修正后的进度估计，无需人工进度标签、奖励标注或额外价值模型。

Result: 在真实双臂布料折叠数据上验证，修正后的标签能捕捉局部回退和非均匀进度，保持整体任务趋势；在匹配数据、模型和训练设置下，用于VLA优势条件策略学习时显示正向任务成功率趋势。

Conclusion: UR-VC通过简单跨轨迹检索有效修正接触丰富操作中的噪声进度信号，无需额外训练或标注，并能提升下游策略学习效果。

Abstract: Modern robot learning systems increasingly rely on dense progress or value signals to evaluate intermediate states, guide policy learning, and detect task completion, making the quality of these signals critical. Since such dense labels are rarely available at scale, normalized time within a demonstration is often used as a scalable substitute: later frames are treated as higher progress. However, this time-derived label is only a noisy proxy for physical task progress. In contact-rich manipulation, a robot may make progress and then lose it through slips, failed grasps, or partial undoing, while the time-derived label continues to increase monotonically. We introduce Unsupervised Robotic Value Correction (UR-VC), an offline, training-free method for correcting time-derived progress labels. UR-VC exploits a simple regularity in demonstration data: similar states often recur across different episodes, but at different timestamps. Instead of trusting the timestamp from a single trajectory, UR-VC retrieves similar states from other episodes and aggregates their time-derived labels to obtain a corrected progress estimate. UR-VC requires no manual progress labels, reward annotations, or additional value model. We evaluate UR-VC on real bimanual cloth flatten-and-fold data, a long-horizon deformable-object manipulation task with visible intermediate progress. The corrected labels capture local regressions and non-uniform progress that normalized time cannot represent, while preserving the overall task trend. We further use the corrected signal to construct advantage labels for VLA training, following recent advantage-conditioned policy learning. UR-VC shows a positive trend in real-robot task success under matched data, model, and training settings.

</details>


### [306] [ExToken: Structured Exploration for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12931)
*Yilun Kong,Yunpeng Qing,Guozheng Ma,Haoyu Wang,Li Shen,Zhi Hou,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RL Exploration Token (ExToken)框架，通过将VLA策略条件化于离线演示得到的离散行为先验，实现结构化探索，显著提升采样效率，在极低交互预算下仍表现鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA-RL框架存在探索停滞瓶颈，且环境交互成本高昂，严重限制了强化学习在视觉-语言-动作模型上的可扩展性。作者发现轨迹多样性比收集的rollout数量对采样效率更为关键。

Method: 提出ExToken框架：1) 从离线演示中学习离散行为先验（行为token）；2) 在rollout收集时通过条件化不同token鼓励智能体探索多样化行为模式；3) 引入状态条件化的token选择器，在部署时自适应预测未见场景的有效行为模式，弥合训练探索与部署确定性推理之间的差距。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作任务上的大量实验表明，ExToken能够持续加速收敛、提升任务性能，并在高度受限的交互预算下表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ExToken是一种简单且通用的框架，通过结构化探索解决了VLA-RL中的样本效率瓶颈，为在有限交互预算下扩展RL在VLA模型上的应用提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has demonstrated significant potential for improving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models on complex manipulation tasks. However, its practical scalability remains severely limited by the substantial cost of environmental interactions. In this work, we first investigate the exploration stagnation bottleneck in current VLA-RL frameworks and reveal that trajectory diversity is fundamentally more important to sample efficiency than the sheer quantity of collected rollouts. Motivated by these insights, we introduce RL Exploration Token (ExToken), a simple yet general framework that condition VLA policies on discrete behavioral priors derived from offline demonstrations for structured exploration. By conditioning the policy on different tokens during rollout collection, ExToken encourages the agent to explore diverse behavioral modes, substantially improving state-action coverage and exploration efficiency. To bridge exploration during training with deterministic inference at deployment, ExToken further incorporates a state-conditioned token selector that adaptively predicts effective behavioral modes for unseen scenarios. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that ExToken consistently accelerates convergence, improves task performance, and exhibits strong robustness under highly constrained interaction budgets.

</details>


### [307] [MAMMOTH: A Multi-Modal End-to-End Policy for Off-Road Mobility Robust to Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12965)
*Ahaan Kotian,Shivani Subramanyan,Suresh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MAMMOTH，一种统一端到端导航策略，通过多模态融合（RGB、热成像、3D点云、速度）和模态丢失训练，结合扩散策略学习物理轨迹与内在可通行性启发式，实现非结构化越野环境下鲁棒的视觉导航，在夜间及传感器退化条件下显著提升避障和地形感知性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越野自主导航方法过度依赖RGB模态，在光照变化（强光、阴影、低光）下表现差；多模态方法则对传感器完美输入有刚性依赖，无法处理传感器退化或单模态失效。

Method: 提出MAMMOTH框架：1) 高效融合多模态观测（RGB、热成像、3D点云、自车速度）；2) 采用模态丢失训练策略，使模型在推理时能泛化到缺失模态；3) 使用扩散策略学习物理轨迹与内在可通行性启发式的联合条件概率分布，偏好更安全平滑的轨迹。

Result: 在多种非结构化越野环境的真实机器人实验中（包括夜间操作），MAMMOTH在避障、地形感知规划和模态缺失泛化方面均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MAMMOTH通过模态丢失训练和可通行性启发式扩散策略，有效解决了多模态导航中对传感器完美输入的刚性依赖问题，在极端环境和传感器退化条件下实现了鲁棒的自主导航。

Abstract: Reliable autonomous navigation in unstructured off-road environments remains a critical unsolved challenge due to extreme terrain diversity, drastic illumination variations and acute sensor degradation. Recent developments have approached the problem as a traversability costmap estimation or visual navigation task. However, many exhibit heavy reliance on RGB modality, leading to poor performance in varied illumination such as glares, shadows or low ambient light. Achieving robust generalization in such conditions requires integrating modalities that provide supplementary scene information. Such multi-modal methods suffer from a rigid dependency on the presence of near-perfect sensor inputs, leaving them unable to robustly handle sensor degradation or individual modality failure. To address these limitations, we introduce MAMMOTH (MAsking Multi-Modal inputs for Off-road Traversability Heuristic-informed navigation), a unified end-to-end navigation policy for robust off-road visual-goal-conditioned navigation and undirected exploration. Specifically, MAMMOTH efficiently fuses multi-modal observations (RGB, Thermal, 3D Pointcloud and Ego Velocity) and is trained with a modality dropout scheme, enabling it to generalize to missing modalities at inference time. Furthermore, we employ a diffusion policy to learn the joint conditional probability distribution of physically-grounded trajectories and a intrinsic traversability heuristic. MAMMOTH utilizes this heuristic to prefer safer, smoother trajectories. We validate MAMMOTH through extensive real-world robot experiments in distinct off-road environments, including night-time operation. Our results demonstrate superior performance, with significant improvements in collision avoidance, terrain-aware planning and generalization to missing modalities. The code and dataset used for this work will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [308] [ChunkFlow: Towards Continuity-Consistent Chunked Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12992)
*Zhao Yang,Yinan Shi,Mingyuan Yao,Wenyao Xue,Yawei Jueluo,Longjun Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChunkFlow提出了一种面向分块动作头边界抖动问题的训练-执行统一框架，通过分区策略、确定性混合和连续性损失训练，在保持低延迟推理的同时提升了VLA模型的时间一致性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型采用分块动作头满足实时性要求，但引入了边界抖动问题：相邻块的重叠区域预测不一致，降低了时间连贯性和任务成功率。现有推理时混合方法仅重新加权不一致的预测而不修正底层错误，在偏置或有噪声的历史条件下存在残差累积。

Method: 将每个分块划分为冻结区、可编辑区和未来区；执行时应用确定性重叠混合；训练时引入接缝损失以及一阶、二阶连续性损失。采用历史破坏和调度采样增强对执行历史误差的鲁棒性，通过AWAC微调阶段适配策略而不移除结构正则化器。

Result: 在CALVIN、LIBERO及真实机器人实验上，ChunkFlow在保持低延迟推理的同时，实现了更好的成功-稳定性权衡。理论分析表明，在轻度平滑性假设下，预混合接缝差异随重叠增加而衰减。

Conclusion: ChunkFlow通过结构化的分块分区和针对性的训练损失，有效解决了分块策略中的边界抖动问题，提升了时间连贯性和任务成功率，并且兼容后续AWAC微调。

Abstract: Vision-language action (VLA) models increasingly adopt chunked action heads to satisfy real-time constraints; however, this introduces boundary jitter: overlapping regions between consecutive chunks often yield inconsistent predictions, degrading temporal coherence and the task success rate. Existing methods, such as inference-time blending, merely reweight mismatched proposals without correcting underlying errors, leading to residual accumulation under biased or noisy histories. We propose ChunkFlow, a seam-aware training-and-execution framework for chunked policies that aligns chunk structure with boundary execution. It partitions each chunk into frozen, editable, and future zones, applies deterministic overlap blending at execution, and trains raw predictions with seam and first- and second-order continuity losses. History corruption and scheduled sampling improve robustness to executed-history errors, while an AWAC fine-tuning stage adapts the policy without removing these structural regularizers. Under mild smoothness assumptions, pre-blending seam discrepancies provably decay with increasing overlap. Experiments on CALVIN, LIBERO, and real robots show an improved success-stability trade-off with low-latency inference. Project page: https://cytoderm-ai.github.io/chunkflow.

</details>


### [309] [FlowWAM: Optical Flow as a Unified Action Representation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13017)
*Yixiang Chen,Peiyan Li,Yuan Xu,Qisen Ma,Jiabing Yang,Kai Wang,Jianhua Yang,Dong An,He Guan,Gaoteng Liu,Jianlou Si,Jun Huang,Jing Liu,Nianfeng Liu,Yan Huang,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowWAM提出了一种基于光流（optical flow）的统一视频原生动作表示方法，用于世界动作模型（WAMs），在策略模式（动作预测）和世界模型模式（未来视频生成）中均取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs中，数值化动作表示难以与预训练视频生成器对齐，而视觉动作表示又忽略了帧间时间运动结构，因此需要一种既能与视频生成器对齐又能携带足够运动信息的统一动作表示。

Method: FlowWAM采用双流扩散框架，将光流作为统一的视频原生动作表示。光流视频与RGB视频格式相同且编码逐像素位移。在策略模式下生成光流用于动作预测，在世界模型模式下使用目标光流序列引导未来视频生成。由于光流可从原始视频中无需动作标签提取，FlowWAM可利用大规模无动作标签视频数据集进行预训练。

Result: 在RoboTwin操控任务上，FlowWAM在Clean和Random设置下分别达到92.94%和92.14%的成功率，优于VLA和WAM基线。在WorldArena世界建模任务中，取得最佳EWMScore（63.71），轨迹准确率相对提升18.4%。

Conclusion: 基于光流的动作表示在策略模式和世界模型模式下均有效，且能利用无标签视频数据进行预训练，为WAMs提供了一种统一的、视频原生且运动信息丰富的动作表示方案。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are able to leverage pretrained video generators for both world modeling and action prediction. However, directly leveraging such video generators for control raises a new challenge: how to represent actions in a suitable form that aligns with pretrained video generators while carrying enough motion cues for accurate control. Existing numerical actions fail to satisfy the former, and prior visual action representations overlook the temporal motion structure across frames. We address this issue with FlowWAM, a dual-stream diffusion framework that adopts optical flow as a unified, video-native action representation. Flow videos share the same format as RGB videos and encode rich per-pixel displacement. By jointly modeling them within a shared pretrained video generator, FlowWAM can naturally implement two modes of WAMs. In policy mode, FlowWAM generates flow for action prediction, while in world-model mode, it uses target flow sequences to guide future video generation. Moreover, since flow can be easily extracted from raw videos without action labels, FlowWAM can leverage large-scale action-unlabeled video datasets for pretraining. We empirically find that our flow-based action representation delivers gains across both modes. On RoboTwin manipulation, FlowWAM raises the success rate to 92.94% on the Clean setting and 92.14% on Random, outperforming both VLA and WAM baselines. On WorldArena world modeling, it achieves the best overall EWMScore (63.71) with an 18.4% relative improvement in trajectory accuracy. More results can be found on our project website: https://flow-wam.github.io .

</details>


### [310] [DenseReward: Dense Reward Learning via Failure Synthesis for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13033)
*Yu Fang,Wanxi Dong,Jiaqi Liu,Yue Yang,Mingxiao Huo,Yao Mu,Huaxiu Yao,Li Erran Li,Daniel Szafir,Mingyu Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DenseReward，一种密集机器人奖励模型，通过自动化仿真故障数据生成流水线合成物理真实故障轨迹，预测密集帧级奖励分数，为下游模型预测控制和强化学习提供有效奖励指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习在改进机器人策略方面潜力巨大，但缺乏可靠且能提供密集信息反馈的视觉语言奖励模型，现有方法面临两个关键挑战：大规模多样化故障数据采集困难，以及现有奖励模型仅提供稀疏二值或轨迹级奖励，难以有效指导策略优化。

Method: 开发自动化故障数据生成流水线，在仿真中无需人工标注即可合成物理真实的故障轨迹，涵盖碰撞、抓取失败、物体掉落和恢复行为等多种故障模式；训练DenseReward模型，从视觉观测和语言指令中预测密集帧级奖励分数，实现细粒度的任务进度估计。

Result: 实验表明，DenseReward在模拟和真实操作场景的密集奖励预测任务中优于通用视觉语言模型和现有机器人奖励模型，并能有效为下游模型预测控制和强化学习提供奖励指导。

Conclusion: DenseReward通过自动化仿真故障数据生成和密集帧级奖励预测，有效解决了机器人强化学习中奖励信号稀疏和故障数据匮乏的问题，将开源数据集、训练好的奖励模型及评估套件以支持该领域发展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning holds great promise for improving robot policies beyond the limits of imitation learning. However, its practical adoption remains bottlenecked by the lack of reliable vision-language reward models that provide dense and informative feedback. Two key challenges remain: acquiring diverse failure data at scale and obtaining fine-grained reward signals beyond sparse trajectory-level success labels. Collecting failure trajectories typically requires laborious human effort, while pseudo-failures constructed by relabeling successful demonstrations fail to capture the diverse physical failure modes that arise during robot execution. Meanwhile, existing reward models often predict sparse binary or trajectory-level rewards, which provide limited guidance for efficient policy optimization. We introduce DenseReward, a dense robotic reward model that addresses both challenges. To train DenseReward, we develop an automated failure data generation pipeline that synthesizes physically realistic failure trajectories in simulation without human labeling, covering diverse failure modes such as collisions, missed grasps, object drops, and recovery behaviors. DenseReward predicts dense frame-level reward scores from visual observations and language instructions, enabling fine-grained estimation of task progress throughout an episode. Experiments show that DenseReward outperforms general-purpose VLMs and existing robotic reward models in dense reward prediction across both simulated and real-world manipulation. We further demonstrate that DenseReward provides effective reward guidance for downstream model predictive control and reinforcement learning. We release the dataset, trained reward models, and evaluation suite to support the development of failure-aware dense reward modeling for robot learning.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [311] [Reading the Eyes in VR: Multimodal Modeling of Social Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11931)
*Mohammad Fahim Abrar,Shayla Sharmin,Roghayeh Leila Barmaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究在桌面和虚拟现实(VR)两种媒介上实施了眼神读心测验(RMET)，并考察了即时反馈对行为的影响。结果发现即时反馈增加了注视时长和脑电参与度，VR虽未显著改变客观指标但获得了更高的可用性评分，同时也被认为更费力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RMET通常在纸质或桌面显示器上呈现，存在被试间观看几何不一致、缺乏即时反馈等问题，影响了测试的标准化和过程测量。本研究旨在探究呈现媒介和试次级即时反馈是否影响RMET行为表现。

Method: 在Unity中实现RMET的桌面版和VR版，VR用于保持刺激距离和视野恒定。开展2x2混合设计实验（N=20），设备（VR vs.桌面）为被试间变量，反馈（即时正确性提示 vs.无反馈）为被试内变量。同步记录眼动追踪、EEG、行为日志和主观问卷数据。

Result: 即时反馈条件下注视时长更长、EEG参与度更高，但任务完成时间和正确总数无显著差异。呈现媒介在客观测量上无可靠差异，但VR获得了更高的可用性评分，同时也被认为更费力。

Conclusion: RMET可以在受控的VR和桌面环境中作为过程感知评估任务进行研究，为后续标准化社交认知测量提供了初步证据。

Abstract: Social intelligence, the ability to interpret others' emotions, beliefs, and intentions, is often assessed with the Reading the Mind in the Eyes Test (RMET), in which participants infer mental states from images of the eye region. Yet RMET is typically presented on paper or desktop displays, where viewing geometry can vary across participants, and it rarely includes immediate feedback. We investigated whether presentation medium and brief trial-level feedback influence RMET behavior. We implemented RMET in Unity for both desktop and Virtual Reality (VR), using VR to hold stimulus distance and field of view constant without changing the items. We conducted a 2x2 mixed study with 20 participants, with device (VR vs. desktop) manipulated between subjects and feedback (immediate correctness cue vs. none) manipulated within subjects. Eye-tracking and EEG data were recorded and synchronized with behavioral logs. We analyzed fixation-based gaze measures, EEG signals, response time, accuracy, and subjective measures. Immediate feedback was associated with longer fixation durations and higher EEG-based engagement, while no significant differences were observed in task completion time or total correct answers. Presentation medium did not produce reliable differences in the objective measures, but VR received higher usability ratings and was also rated as more effortful. These results provide initial evidence that RMET can be studied as a process-aware assessment task in controlled VR and desktop settings.

</details>


### [312] [Thought Experiments for Conceptual Work: A New Application of a (Very) Old Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12092)
*Leah Hope Ajmani,Mo Houtti,Eric P. S. Baumer,Stevie Chancellor*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出思想实验（TEs）作为人机交互（HCI）研究者进行概念性工作的关键方法，通过逻辑推演将概念化引入其规范性含义，以预见技术的潜在危害，并响应HCI领域对研究伦理和更广泛影响的审查呼吁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HCI作为跨学科领域常以概念作为理论构建基础，但这些概念承诺带有规范性后果。目前HCI缺乏系统的方法来审视概念化带来的伦理影响，需要一种能够将概念推演至其规范性含义的方法。

Method: 将思想实验（TEs）作为一种质疑性（interrogative）方法引入HCI，通过构建假设但可控的场景，经由逻辑推演将概念化导向其规范性含义。具体通过两个案例说明：1）原创思想实验批判价值敏感设计中的利益相关者概念；2）分析Helen Nissenbaum利用思想实验生成语境完整性理论的过程。

Result: 思想实验能够精确预见技术的潜在危害，使HCI能够将当前对研究伦理和更广泛影响的审查呼吁具体化、可操作化。

Conclusion: 思想实验是HCI研究者进行概念性工作的有效方法，有助于在技术设计早期识别和规避潜在的伦理问题，推动HCI领域对研究伦理和更广泛社会影响的深入审视。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose thought experiments (TEs) as a crucial method for Human-Computer Interaction (HCI) researchers to engage in conceptual work. As an interdisciplinary field, HCI often uses concepts as the fundamental building blocks for larger theories. However, the conceptual commitments we make in this process carry normative consequences.
